end

gui:SetAttribute("OutlineColor",accent)

gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
	if object:IsA("UIStroke") then
		task.defer(function()
			if object and object.Parent then
				object.Color = gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent
			end
		end)
	end
end)

local function corner(object,radius)
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,radius or 10)
	c.Parent = object
	return c
end
local function addStroke(object,color,thickness,transparency)
	local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	s.Color = color or accent
	s.Thickness = thickness or 1
	s.Transparency = transparency or 0
	s.Parent = object
	return s
end

local function setStatus(text)
	if not status or not status.Parent then
		return
	end
	status.Text = text
	status.TextColor3 = accent

	local notifyEvent = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyNotifyEvent")
	if notifyEvent and notifyEvent:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		notifyEvent:Fire(tostring(text))
	end
end

local pages = {}
local tabButtons = {}
local currentTab = "HOME"
local function makePage(name)
	local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	page.Name = name.."Page"
	page.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	page.BorderSizePixel = 0
	page.ScrollBarThickness = 4
	page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
	page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
	page.Visible = false
	page.Parent = content
	local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,10)
	pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0,12)
	pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,10)
	pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,10)
	pad.Parent = page
	local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	layout.Padding = UDim.new(0,8)
	layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	layout.Parent = page
	pages[name] = page
	return page
end
local function makeTab(name,text)
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	button.Name = name.."Tab"
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42)
	button.Text = text
	button.TextSize = 10
	button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	button.TextColor3 = TEXT
	button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.Parent = sidebar
	corner(button,10)

	local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	scale.Scale = 1
	scale.Parent = button

	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.08,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = .96}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	tabButtons[name] = button
	return button
end
local function applyTabVisuals()
	for name,button in pairs(tabButtons) do
		local selected = name == currentTab
		TweenService:Create(
			button,
			TweenInfo.new(.14,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{
				BackgroundColor3 = selected and accent or SURFACE,
				TextColor3 = selected and ACTIVE_TEXT or TEXT
			}
		):Play()
	end

	for name,page in pairs(pages) do
		page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent

		if name == currentTab then
			page.Visible = true
			page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,0)
			page.ScrollBarImageTransparency = .55

			TweenService:Create(
				page,
				TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0),
					ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0
				}
			):Play()
		else
			page.Visible = false
		end
	end
end
local function setTab(name)
	if not pages[name] then
		return
	end
	currentTab = name
	applyTabVisuals()
	local displayName = name
	if name == "LOCUST" then
		displayName = "HOUSE OF THE LOCUST"
	elseif name == "HOME" then
		displayName = "UNIVERSAL"
	elseif name == "CREATOR" then
		displayName = "CREATOR"
	elseif name == "DASHBOARD" then
		displayName = "DASHBOARD"
	elseif name == "EXPLOITER" then
		displayName = "EXPLOITER DETECTOR"
	elseif name == "TROLL" then
		displayName = "TROLL"
	end
	setStatus(displayName)
end
makeTab("HOME","UNIVERSAL").Activated:Connect(function() setTab("HOME") end)
makeTab("MOVEMENT","MOVEMENT").Activated:Connect(function() setTab("MOVEMENT") end)
makeTab("VISUALS","VISUALS").Activated:Connect(function() setTab("VISUALS") end)
makeTab("TRACKERS","TRACKERS").Activated:Connect(function() setTab("TRACKERS") end)
makeTab("LOCUST","HOUSE OF LOCUST").Activated:Connect(function() setTab("LOCUST") end)
makeTab("SETTINGS","SETTINGS").Activated:Connect(function() setTab("SETTINGS") end)
makeTab("TROLL","TROLL").Activated:Connect(function() setTab("TROLL") end)

makePage("TROLL")
local function makeSection(page,titleText,description)
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,description and 66 or 48)
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	frame.Parent = page
	corner(frame,12)
	local sectionTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	sectionTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	sectionTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,8)
	sectionTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,20)
	sectionTitle.Text = titleText
	sectionTitle.TextSize = 11
	sectionTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	sectionTitle.TextColor3 = TEXT
	sectionTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	sectionTitle.Parent = frame
	if description then
		local desc = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		desc.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,29)
		desc.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,29)
		desc.Text = description
		desc.TextWrapped = true
		desc.TextSize = 9
		desc.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		desc.TextColor3 = MUTED
		desc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		desc.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		desc.Parent = frame
	end
	return frame
end
local function makeAction(page,text)
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
	button.Text = text
	button.TextSize = 10
	button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	button.TextColor3 = TEXT
	button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.Parent = page
	corner(button,11)

	local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	scale.Parent = button

	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.07,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = .975}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	return button
end
local function makePair(page,leftText,rightText)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
	row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	row.Parent = page
	local left = Instance.new("TextButton")
	left.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
	left.Text = leftText
	left.TextSize = 10
	left.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	left.TextColor3 = TEXT
	left.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	left.AutoButtonColor = false
	left.Parent = row
	corner(left,11)
	local right = Instance.new("TextButton")
	right.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
	right.Position = UDim2.new(.5,4,0,0)
	right.Text = rightText
	right.TextSize = 10
	right.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	right.TextColor3 = TEXT
	right.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	right.AutoButtonColor = false
	right.Parent = row
	corner(right,11)

	for _,button in ipairs({left,right}) do
		local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		scale.Parent = button

		button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(.07,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = .97}
				):Play()
			end
		end)

		button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = 1}
				):Play()
			end
		end)
	end

	return left,right
end
local function makeNumberRow(page,labelText,defaultText,buttonText)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,48)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.Parent = page
	corner(row,11)
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,0)
	label.Size = UDim2.new(.30,-6,1,0)
	label.Text = labelText
	label.TextSize = 9
	label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	label.TextColor3 = TEXT
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	label.Parent = row
	local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
	box.Size = UDim2.new(.25,-5,0,34)
	box.Position = UDim2.new(.30,0,.5,-17)
	box.Text = defaultText
	box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	box.TextSize = 11
	box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	box.TextColor3 = TEXT
	box.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	box.Parent = row
	corner(box,9)
	local apply = Instance.new("TextButton")
	apply.Size = UDim2.new(.45,-12,0,34)
	apply.Position = UDim2.new(.55,5,.5,-17)
	apply.Text = buttonText or "APPLY"
	apply.TextSize = 9
	apply.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	apply.TextColor3 = TEXT
	apply.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	apply.AutoButtonColor = false
	apply.Parent = row
	corner(apply,9)

	local applyScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	applyScale.Parent = apply
	local boxStroke = addStroke(box,accent,1.2,1)

	apply.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				applyScale,
				TweenInfo.new(.07,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = .96}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	apply.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			TweenService:Create(
				applyScale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end
	end)

	box.Focused:Connect(function()
		boxStroke.Color = accent
		TweenService:Create(
			boxStroke,
			TweenInfo.new(.12),
			{Transparency = .1}
		):Play()
	end)

	box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		TweenService:Create(
			boxStroke,
			TweenInfo.new(.16),
			{Transparency = 1}
		):Play()
	end)

	return box,apply
end

local function showPlayerPicker(titleText,onSelected,includeSelf)
	local oldPicker = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyPlayerPicker")
	if oldPicker then
		oldPicker:Destroy()
	end

	local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
	overlay.Name = "NotRubyPlayerPicker"
	overlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
	overlay.BackgroundTransparency = .28
	overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
	overlay.Active = true
	overlay.ZIndex = 950
	overlay.Parent = gui

	local card = Instance.new("Frame")
	card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
	card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
	card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(410,390)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = BG
	card.BorderSizePixel = 0
	card.ZIndex = 951
	card.Parent = overlay
	corner(card,18)
	addStroke(card,accent,1.8,.02)

	local cardScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	cardScale.Scale = .9
	cardScale.Parent = card
	TweenService:Create(
		cardScale,
		TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{Scale = 1}
	):Play()

	local pickerTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	pickerTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	pickerTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16,12)
	pickerTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-64,0,30)
	pickerTitle.Text = titleText or "SELECT PLAYER"
	pickerTitle.TextSize = 14
	pickerTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	pickerTitle.TextColor3 = TEXT
	pickerTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	pickerTitle.ZIndex = 952
	pickerTitle.Parent = card

	local closePicker = Instance.new("TextButton")
	closePicker.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34,34)
	closePicker.Position = UDim2.new(1,-46,0,10)
	closePicker.Text = "X"
	closePicker.TextSize = 13
	closePicker.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	closePicker.TextColor3 = TEXT
	closePicker.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(92,32,43)
	closePicker.AutoButtonColor = false
	closePicker.ZIndex = 952
	closePicker.Parent = card
	corner(closePicker,9)

	local search = Instance.new("TextBox")
	search.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,52)
	search.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,38)
	search.PlaceholderText = "Search username or display name"
	search.Text = ""
	search.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	search.TextSize = 10
	search.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	search.TextColor3 = TEXT
	search.PlaceholderColor3 = MUTED
	search.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	search.BorderSizePixel = 0
	search.ZIndex = 952
	search.Parent = card
	corner(search,10)

	local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,100)
	list.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-114)
	list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	list.BorderSizePixel = 0
	list.ScrollBarThickness = 3
	list.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
	list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
	list.ZIndex = 952
	list.Parent = card

	local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,7)
	listLayout.Parent = list

	local rows = {}

	local function close()
		if overlay and overlay.Parent then
			overlay:Destroy()
		end
	end

	local function addPlayerRow(otherPlayer)
		if otherPlayer == player and not includeSelf then
			return
		end

		local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
		row.Name = "Player_"..tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,62)
		row.Text = ""
		row.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
		row.BorderSizePixel = 0
		row.AutoButtonColor = false
		row.ZIndex = 953
		row.Parent = list
		corner(row,11)

		local rowScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		rowScale.Parent = row

		local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		avatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46,46)
		avatar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8,8)
		avatar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		avatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
		avatar.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
		avatar.ZIndex = 954
		avatar.Parent = row
		corner(avatar,23)
		addStroke(avatar,accent,1.2,.12)

		local displayName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		displayName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		displayName.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(64,9)
		displayName.Size = UDim2.new(1,-76,0,20)
		displayName.Text = otherPlayer.DisplayName
		displayName.TextSize = 11
		displayName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		displayName.TextColor3 = TEXT
		displayName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		displayName.ZIndex = 954
		displayName.Parent = row

		local username = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		username.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		username.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(64,30)
		username.Size = UDim2.new(1,-76,0,18)
		username.Text = "@"..otherPlayer.Name.."  •  "..tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
		username.TextSize = 9
		username.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		username.TextColor3 = MUTED
		username.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		username.ZIndex = 954
		username.Parent = row

		rows[row] = {
			searchText = string.lower(otherPlayer.Name.." "..otherPlayer.DisplayName)
		}

		row.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					rowScale,
					TweenInfo.new(.07),
					{Scale = .97}
				):Play()
			end
		end)

		row.InputEnded:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(
				rowScale,
				TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{Scale = 1}
			):Play()
		end)

		row.Activated:Connect(function()
			close()
			if onSelected then
				onSelected(otherPlayer)
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,image = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
					otherPlayer.UserId,
					Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
					Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
				)
			end)
			if ok and image and avatar.Parent then
				avatar.Image = image
			end
		end)
	end

	for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		addPlayerRow(otherPlayer)
	end

	search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local query = string.lower(search.Text)
		for row,data in pairs(rows) do
			if row and row.Parent then
				row.Visible =
					query == ""
					or string.find(data.searchText,query,1,true) ~= nil
			end
		end
	end)

	closePicker.Activated:Connect(close)
end

local function setupCreatorTab()
	local creatorTab = makeTab("CREATOR","CREATOR")
	local creatorPage = makePage("CREATOR")

	local creatorUsername = "werdx33xxxr5"
	local creatorDisplayName = "not ruby"
	local creatorUserId = 5717888103
	local creatorPassword = "Not ruby v1 the best"
	local creatorUnlockFile =
		"not_ruby_v4_creator_unlock_"..tostring(player.UserId)..".txt"

	local unlocked = false
	local cornerClicks = 0
	local lastCornerClick = 0

	local function hasRememberedCreatorAccess()
		if type(isfile) ~= "function"
			or type(readfile) ~= "function" then
			return false
		end

		local ok,result = pcall(function()
			if not isfile(creatorUnlockFile) then
				return false
			end

			return tostring(readfile(creatorUnlockFile))
				== tostring(player.UserId)
		end)

		return ok and result == true
	end

	local function rememberCreatorAccess()
		if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
			return false
		end

		local ok = pcall(function()
			writefile(
				creatorUnlockFile,
				tostring(player.UserId)
			)
		end)

		return ok
	end

	makeSection(
		creatorPage,
		"CREATOR",
		"Creator identity hidden."
	)

	local card = Instance.new("Frame")
	card.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,255)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	card.BorderSizePixel = 0
	card.Parent = creatorPage
	corner(card,14)
	addStroke(
		card,
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.2,
		.08
	)

	local logo = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	logo.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,0)
	logo.Position = UDim2.new(.5,0,0,18)
	logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104,104)
	logo.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	logo.BorderSizePixel = 0
	logo.Image = ""
	logo.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
	logo.Parent = card
	corner(logo,52)
	addStroke(
		logo,
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.5,
		.05
	)

	local hiddenMark = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	hiddenMark.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	hiddenMark.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	hiddenMark.Text = "?"
	hiddenMark.TextSize = 42
	hiddenMark.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	hiddenMark.TextColor3 = accent
	hiddenMark.Parent = logo

	local display = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	display.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	display.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,132)
	display.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,27)
	display.Text = "HIDDEN"
	display.TextSize = 17
	display.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	display.TextColor3 = TEXT
	display.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	display.Parent = card

	local username = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	username.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	username.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,161)
	username.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,21)
	username.Text = "RESTRICTED ACCESS"
	username.TextSize = 10
	username.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	username.TextColor3 = accent
	username.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	username.Parent = card

	local userIdLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	userIdLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	userIdLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,184)
	userIdLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,20)
	userIdLabel.Text = "CREATOR PROFILE IS PRIVATE"
	userIdLabel.TextSize = 9
	userIdLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	userIdLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
	userIdLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	userIdLabel.Parent = card

	local accessLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	accessLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	accessLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,214)
	accessLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,22)
	accessLabel.Text = "HIDDEN / RESTRICTED ACCESS"
	accessLabel.TextSize = 8
	accessLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	accessLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
	accessLabel.TextWrapped = true
	accessLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	accessLabel.Parent = card

	-- Invisible secret corner. Tap/click the top-right of the big hidden card 5 times.
	-- Parent it to the card so the Creator page UIListLayout cannot move it.
	local secretCorner = Instance.new("TextButton")
	secretCorner.Name = "SecretCreatorCorner"
	secretCorner.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
	secretCorner.Position = UDim2.new(1,-8,0,8)
	secretCorner.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72,72)
	secretCorner.Text = ""
	secretCorner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	secretCorner.BorderSizePixel = 0
	secretCorner.AutoButtonColor = false
	secretCorner.Active = true
	secretCorner.ZIndex = 80
	secretCorner.Parent = card

	local function showCreator()
		unlocked = true

		hiddenMark.Visible = false
		display.Text = creatorDisplayName
		username.Text = "@"..creatorUsername
		userIdLabel.Text = "USER ID: "..tostring(creatorUserId)
		accessLabel.Text = "SECRET ACCESS GRANTED"

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,image = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
					creatorUserId,
					Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
					Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420
				)
			end)

			if ok and image and logo.Parent then
				logo.Image = image
			end
		end)
	end

	local function openPasswordPrompt()
		if unlocked then
			return
		end

		local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
		overlay.Name = "CreatorPasswordPrompt"
		overlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
		overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
		overlay.BackgroundTransparency = .28
		overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
		overlay.ZIndex = 900
		overlay.Parent = gui

		local prompt = Instance.new("Frame")
		prompt.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
		prompt.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
		prompt.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(330,205)
		prompt.BackgroundColor3 = BG
		prompt.BorderSizePixel = 0
		prompt.ZIndex = 901
		prompt.Parent = overlay
		corner(prompt,16)
		addStroke(
			prompt,
			gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
			1.6,
			.02
		)

		local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16,14)
		title.Size = UDim2.new(1,-32,0,24)
		title.Text = "RESTRICTED CREATOR ACCESS"
		title.TextSize = 12
		title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		title.TextColor3 = TEXT
		title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		title.ZIndex = 902
		title.Parent = prompt

		local info = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		info.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		info.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16,42)
		info.Size = UDim2.new(1,-32,0,24)
		info.Text = "ENTER PASSWORD"
		info.TextSize = 9
		info.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		info.TextColor3 = MUTED
		info.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		info.ZIndex = 902
		info.Parent = prompt

		local passwordBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
		passwordBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,74)
		passwordBox.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,42)
		passwordBox.Text = ""
		passwordBox.PlaceholderText = "Password"
		passwordBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
		passwordBox.TextSize = 11
		passwordBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		passwordBox.TextColor3 = TEXT
		passwordBox.PlaceholderColor3 = MUTED
		passwordBox.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		passwordBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
		passwordBox.ZIndex = 902
		passwordBox.Parent = prompt
		corner(passwordBox,10)
		addStroke(
			passwordBox,
			gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
			1.1,
			.08
		)

		local submit = Instance.new("TextButton")
		submit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,127)
		submit.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-23,0,42)
		submit.Text = "UNLOCK"
		submit.TextSize = 10
		submit.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		submit.TextColor3 = ACTIVE_TEXT
		submit.BackgroundColor3 = accent
		submit.BorderSizePixel = 0
		submit.AutoButtonColor = false
		submit.ZIndex = 902
		submit.Parent = prompt
		corner(submit,10)

		local cancel = Instance.new("TextButton")
		cancel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
		cancel.Position = UDim2.new(1,-18,0,127)
		cancel.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-23,0,42)
		cancel.Text = "CANCEL"
		cancel.TextSize = 10
		cancel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		cancel.TextColor3 = TEXT
		cancel.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		cancel.BorderSizePixel = 0
		cancel.AutoButtonColor = false
		cancel.ZIndex = 902
		cancel.Parent = prompt
		corner(cancel,10)

		local message = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		message.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		message.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16,174)
		message.Size = UDim2.new(1,-32,0,18)
		message.Text = ""
		message.TextSize = 8
		message.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		message.TextColor3 = accent
		message.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		message.ZIndex = 902
		message.Parent = prompt

		local function tryPassword()
			if passwordBox.Text == creatorPassword then
				message.Text = "ACCESS GRANTED"
				rememberCreatorAccess()
				task.wait(.18)
				if overlay and overlay.Parent then
					overlay:Destroy()
				end
				showCreator()
				accessLabel.Text = "ACCESS REMEMBERED // WELCOME BACK NEXT TIME"
				setStatus("CREATOR ACCESS GRANTED + REMEMBERED")
			else
				message.Text = "INCORRECT PASSWORD"
				passwordBox.Text = ""
			end
		end

		submit.Activated:Connect(tryPassword)
		passwordBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
			if enterPressed then
				tryPassword()
			end
		end)

		cancel.Activated:Connect(function()
			if overlay and overlay.Parent then
				overlay:Destroy()
			end
		end)

		task.defer(function()
			passwordBox:CaptureFocus()
		end)
	end

	secretCorner.Activated:Connect(function()
		if unlocked then
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()

		-- If the user waits too long, restart the hidden 5-click sequence.
		if now - lastCornerClick > 5 then
			cornerClicks = 0
		end

		lastCornerClick = now
		cornerClicks += 1

		if cornerClicks >= 5 then
			cornerClicks = 0
			openPasswordPrompt()
		end
	end)

	creatorTab.Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("CREATOR")
	end)

	-- Once the correct password has been entered on this account/device,
	-- open Creator automatically on future loads when file persistence exists.
	if hasRememberedCreatorAccess() then
		showCreator()
		accessLabel.Text = "WELCOME BACK // CREATOR ACCESS REMEMBERED"
	end
end
setupCreatorTab()


local function setupFinalAdminTabs()
	local dashboardTab = makeTab("DASHBOARD","DASHBOARD")
	local dashboardPage = makePage("DASHBOARD")
	dashboardTab.Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("DASHBOARD")
	end)

	makeSection(
		dashboardPage,
		"NOT RUBY V1",
		"Quick navigation, live character status and server overview."
	)

	local dashCard = Instance.new("Frame")
	dashCard.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,96)
	dashCard.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	dashCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
	dashCard.Parent = dashboardPage
	corner(dashCard,12)

	local dashAvatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	dashAvatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(68,68)
	dashAvatar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,14)
	dashAvatar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	dashAvatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	dashAvatar.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
	dashAvatar.Parent = dashCard
	corner(dashAvatar,34)
	addStroke(dashAvatar,accent,1.4,.08)

	local dashName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	dashName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	dashName.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(94,17)
	dashName.Size = UDim2.new(1,-108,0,22)
	dashName.Text = player.DisplayName
	dashName.TextSize = 13
	dashName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	dashName.TextColor3 = TEXT
	dashName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	dashName.Parent = dashCard

	local dashUser = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	dashUser.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	dashUser.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(94,41)
	dashUser.Size = UDim2.new(1,-108,0,18)
	dashUser.Text = "@"..player.Name
	dashUser.TextSize = 9
	dashUser.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	dashUser.TextColor3 = MUTED
	dashUser.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	dashUser.Parent = dashCard

	local dashServer = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	dashServer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	dashServer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(94,61)
	dashServer.Size = UDim2.new(1,-108,0,18)
	dashServer.Text = "PLAYERS: "..tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
	dashServer.TextSize = 9
	dashServer.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	dashServer.TextColor3 = accent
	dashServer.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	dashServer.Parent = dashCard

	task.spawn(function()
		local ok,image = pcall(function()
			return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
				player.UserId,
				Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
				Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
			)
		end)
		if ok and image and dashAvatar.Parent then
			dashAvatar.Image = image
		end
	end)

	local goUniversal,goTrackers =
		makePair(dashboardPage,"UNIVERSAL","TRACKERS")
	local goExploiter,goSettings =
		makePair(dashboardPage,"EXPLOITER DETECTOR","SETTINGS")

	goUniversal.Activated:Connect(function() setTab("HOME") end)
	goTrackers.Activated:Connect(function() setTab("TRACKERS") end)
	goExploiter.Activated:Connect(function() setTab("EXPLOITER") end)
	goSettings.Activated:Connect(function() setTab("SETTINGS") end)

	local liveStatus = makeSection(
		dashboardPage,
		"LIVE CHARACTER",
		"Waiting for character data..."
	)

	task.spawn(function()
		while not menuDeleted do
			task.wait(.35)
			if dashServer and dashServer.Parent then
				dashServer.Text = "PLAYERS: "..tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
			end
			if currentTab == "DASHBOARD" and liveStatus and liveStatus.Parent then
				local labels = {}
				for _,child in ipairs(liveStatus:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
						table.insert(labels,child)
					end
				end
				if #labels >= 2 then
					local health = humanoid and math.floor(humanoid.Health + .5) or 0
					local state = "UNKNOWN"

					if humanoid then
						local rawState = humanoid:GetState()
						local moving = humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > .05
						local onGround = humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
						local verticalVelocity =
							root and root.Parent
							and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
							or 0

						if rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
							or (not onGround and verticalVelocity > 1) then
							state = "JUMPING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
							or (not onGround and verticalVelocity <= 1) then
							state = "FALLING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing then
							state = "CLIMBING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
							state = "SWIMMING"
						elseif rawState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated then
							state = "SITTING"
						elseif moving and onGround then
							state = "WALKING"
						elseif onGround then
							state = "IDLE"
						else
							state = string.upper(rawState.Name)
						end
					end

					local pos = root and root.Parent and root.Position or Vector3.zero
					labels[2].Text = string.format(
						"HP: %d   |   State: %s   |   Position: %d, %d, %d",
						health,
						state,
						math.floor(pos.X),
						math.floor(pos.Y),
						math.floor(pos.Z)
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local exploiterTab = makeTab("EXPLOITER","EXPLOITER DETECTOR")
	local exploiterPage = makePage("EXPLOITER")
	exploiterTab.Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("EXPLOITER")
	end)

	makeSection(
		exploiterPage,
		"EXPLOITER DETECTOR",
		"Select a player. Normal mode learns game speed. STRICT BASEPLATE MODE is for simple games and also watches replicated fling physics, force objects, abnormal jumps, noclip-like paths, flight and teleports."
	)

	local selectPlayerButton = makeAction(exploiterPage,"SELECT PLAYER")
	local strictDetectorButton =
		makeAction(exploiterPage,"STRICT BASEPLATE MODE [OFF]")
	local strictBaseplateMode = false

	local function renderStrictDetector()
		strictDetectorButton.Text =
			strictBaseplateMode
			and "STRICT BASEPLATE MODE [ON]"
			or "STRICT BASEPLATE MODE [OFF]"
		strictDetectorButton.BackgroundColor3 =
			strictBaseplateMode and accent or SURFACE_2
		strictDetectorButton.TextColor3 =
			strictBaseplateMode
			and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
			or TEXT
	end

	strictDetectorButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		strictBaseplateMode = not strictBaseplateMode
		renderStrictDetector()
		setStatus(
			strictBaseplateMode
			and "STRICT BASEPLATE DETECTION ON"
			or "STRICT BASEPLATE DETECTION OFF"
		)
	end)

	renderStrictDetector()

	local selectedCard = Instance.new("Frame")
	selectedCard.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,172)
	selectedCard.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	selectedCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
	selectedCard.Parent = exploiterPage
	corner(selectedCard,12)

	local inspectAvatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	inspectAvatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(66,66)
	inspectAvatar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,12)
	inspectAvatar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	inspectAvatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	inspectAvatar.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
	inspectAvatar.Parent = selectedCard
	corner(inspectAvatar,33)
	addStroke(inspectAvatar,accent,1.4,.08)

	local selectedTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	selectedTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectedTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(88,12)
	selectedTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-100,0,22)
	selectedTitle.Text = "NO PLAYER SELECTED"
	selectedTitle.TextSize = 11
	selectedTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	selectedTitle.TextColor3 = TEXT
	selectedTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	selectedTitle.Parent = selectedCard

	local selectedUser = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	selectedUser.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectedUser.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(88,35)
	selectedUser.Size = UDim2.new(1,-100,0,18)
	selectedUser.Text = "--"
	selectedUser.TextSize = 9
	selectedUser.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	selectedUser.TextColor3 = MUTED
	selectedUser.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	selectedUser.Parent = selectedCard

	local verdictLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	verdictLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	verdictLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(88,55)
	verdictLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-100,0,18)
	verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: --"
	verdictLabel.TextSize = 9
	verdictLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	verdictLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
	verdictLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	verdictLabel.Parent = selectedCard

	local executorLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	executorLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	executorLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,88)
	executorLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,22)
	executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: --"
	executorLabel.TextSize = 10
	executorLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	executorLabel.TextColor3 = TEXT
	executorLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	executorLabel.Parent = selectedCard

	local signalsLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	signalsLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	signalsLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12,113)
	signalsLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,48)
	signalsLabel.Text = "SIGNALS: --"
	signalsLabel.TextWrapped = true
	signalsLabel.TextSize = 9
	signalsLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	signalsLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
	signalsLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	signalsLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	signalsLabel.Parent = selectedCard

	local refreshInspectButton,clearInspectButton =
		makePair(exploiterPage,"REFRESH","CLEAR PLAYER")

	makeSection(
		exploiterPage,
		"EXECUTOR LABELS",
		"Server labels recognized: Delta, Wave, Arceus X, Codex, Fluxus and Hydrogen. Other reported names are also shown."
	)

	local selectedPlayer = nil
	local lastPosition = nil
	local lastSampleTime = nil
	local airborneTime = 0
	local signalCounts = {SPEED = 0,TELEPORT = 0,AIR = 0,HOVER = 0,UPWARD = 0,FLING = 0,FORCE = 0,JUMP = 0,NOCLIP = 0}
	local executorAliases = {
		delta = "DELTA",
		wave = "WAVE",
		arceus = "ARCEUS X",
		["arceus x"] = "ARCEUS X",
		codex = "CODEX",
		fluxus = "FLUXUS",
		hydrogen = "HYDROGEN"
	}

	local function normalizeExecutorName(value)
		if typeof(value) ~= "string" then
			return nil
		end
		local cleaned = string.gsub(value,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
		if cleaned == "" then
			return nil
		end
		return executorAliases[string.lower(cleaned)] or string.upper(cleaned)
	end

	local function resetInspectorHistory()
		lastPosition = nil
		lastSampleTime = nil
		airborneTime = 0
		signalCounts.SPEED = 0
		signalCounts.TELEPORT = 0
		signalCounts.AIR = 0
		signalCounts.HOVER = 0
		signalCounts.UPWARD = 0
		signalCounts.FLING = 0
		signalCounts.FORCE = 0
		signalCounts.JUMP = 0
		signalCounts.NOCLIP = 0
	end

	local function getGameWalkSpeedBaseline()
		local speeds = {}

		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local otherCharacter = otherPlayer.Character
			local otherHumanoid =
				otherCharacter
				and otherCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if otherHumanoid and otherHumanoid.Health > 0 then
				table.insert(speeds,otherHumanoid.WalkSpeed)
			end
		end

		if #speeds == 0 then
			return 16
		end

		table.sort(speeds)

		-- Use the upper-middle server speed instead of assuming Roblox's
		-- default 16. If the game intentionally makes everybody faster,
		-- that becomes the detector's normal baseline.
		local index = math.clamp(
			math.ceil(#speeds * .75),
			1,
			#speeds
		)

		return math.max(16,speeds[index] or 16)
	end

	local function getGameJumpBaseline()
		local values = {}

		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local otherCharacter = otherPlayer.Character
			local otherHumanoid =
				otherCharacter
				and otherCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if otherHumanoid and otherHumanoid.Health > 0 then
				local value =
					otherHumanoid.UseJumpPower
					and otherHumanoid.JumpPower
					or otherHumanoid.JumpHeight
				table.insert(values,value)
			end
		end

		if #values == 0 then
			return 50
		end

		table.sort(values)
		local index =
			math.clamp(math.ceil(#values*.75),1,#values)

		return values[index] or 50
	end

	local function hasCharacterForceObject(selectedCharacter)
		for _,object in ipairs(selectedCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("BodyVelocity")
				or object:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity")
				or object:IsA("BodyPosition")
				or object:IsA("BodyGyro")
				or object:IsA("LinearVelocity")
				or object:IsA("AngularVelocity")
				or object:IsA("VectorForce")
				or object:IsA("AlignPosition")
				or object:IsA("AlignOrientation") then
				return true,object.ClassName
			end
		end

		return false,nil
	end

	local function hasGroundBelow(selectedCharacter,selectedRoot,distance)
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {selectedCharacter}

		local result = workspace:Raycast(
			selectedRoot.Position,
			Vector3.new(0,-math.max(4,distance or 12),0),
			params
		)

		return result ~= nil
	end

	local function getSelectedParts()
		if not selectedPlayer or not selectedPlayer.Parent then
			return nil,nil,nil
		end
		local selectedCharacter = selectedPlayer.Character
		if not selectedCharacter then
			return nil,nil,nil
		end
		local selectedHumanoid = selectedCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local selectedRoot =
			selectedCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			or selectedCharacter.PrimaryPart
		return selectedCharacter,selectedHumanoid,selectedRoot
	end

	local function updateInspection()
		if not selectedPlayer or not selectedPlayer.Parent then
			selectedTitle.Text = "NO PLAYER SELECTED"
			selectedUser.Text = "--"
			inspectAvatar.Image = ""
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: --"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: --"
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = TEXT
			signalsLabel.Text = "SIGNALS: --"
			return
		end

		selectedTitle.Text = selectedPlayer.DisplayName
		selectedUser.Text = "@"..selectedPlayer.Name.."  •  "..tostring(selectedPlayer.UserId)

		local selectedCharacter,selectedHumanoid,selectedRoot = getSelectedParts()
		if not selectedCharacter or not selectedRoot then
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: CHARACTER UNAVAILABLE"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = MUTED
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: --"
			signalsLabel.Text = "SIGNALS: waiting for character"
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		if lastPosition and lastSampleTime then
			local dt = math.max(now - lastSampleTime,.001)
			local delta = selectedRoot.Position - lastPosition
			local horizontal =
				Vector3.new(delta.X,0,delta.Z).Magnitude / dt

			local gameWalkSpeed = getGameWalkSpeedBaseline()
			local playerWalkSpeed =
				selectedHumanoid
				and math.max(selectedHumanoid.WalkSpeed,0)
				or gameWalkSpeed

			-- GAME-SPEED-AWARE CHECK:
			-- Fast movement by itself is not enough anymore. The selected
			-- player's WalkSpeed must ALSO be a clear outlier compared with
			-- the rest of the server. This stops games with naturally high
			-- movement speed from being called suspicious.
			local walkSpeedOutlier =
				playerWalkSpeed > (
					strictBaseplateMode
					and math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 1.35,
						gameWalkSpeed + 8
					)
					or math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 1.65,
						gameWalkSpeed + 20
					)
				)

			local movementTooFast =
				horizontal > (
					strictBaseplateMode
					and math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 2.35,
						playerWalkSpeed * 1.7,
						52
					)
					or math.max(
						gameWalkSpeed * 3.5,
						playerWalkSpeed * 2.25,
						110
					)
				)

			if walkSpeedOutlier and movementTooFast then
				signalCounts.SPEED += 1
			else
				-- Old spikes slowly clear instead of permanently marking
				-- somebody suspicious after one lag/game-speed burst.
				signalCounts.SPEED =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.SPEED - 1)
			end

			local teleportDistance =
				strictBaseplateMode and 75 or 180

			if delta.Magnitude > teleportDistance
				and dt < .65 then
				signalCounts.TELEPORT += 1
			end
			local velocity = selectedRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			local angularVelocity =
				selectedRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude

			-- Very high spin/velocity is common with fling/physics scripts.
			local flingLinearThreshold =
				strictBaseplateMode and 95 or 165
			local flingAngularThreshold =
				strictBaseplateMode and 18 or 42

			if velocity.Magnitude >= flingLinearThreshold
				or angularVelocity >= flingAngularThreshold then
				signalCounts.FLING += 1
			else
				signalCounts.FLING =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.FLING - 1)
			end

			-- In a simple/baseplate game, character force objects are a useful
			-- extra clue. They are NOT treated as proof by themselves outside
			-- strict mode because legitimate games can also use constraints.
			local hasForceObject =
				hasCharacterForceObject(selectedCharacter)

			if hasForceObject then
				signalCounts.FORCE +=
					strictBaseplateMode and 1 or .25
			else
				signalCounts.FORCE =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.FORCE - .5)
			end

			if selectedHumanoid then
				local gameJump = getGameJumpBaseline()
				local selectedJump =
					selectedHumanoid.UseJumpPower
					and selectedHumanoid.JumpPower
					or selectedHumanoid.JumpHeight

				local jumpLimit =
					strictBaseplateMode
					and math.max(gameJump*1.35,gameJump+10)
					or math.max(gameJump*1.8,gameJump+30)

				if selectedJump > jumpLimit then
					signalCounts.JUMP += 1
				else
					signalCounts.JUMP =
						math.max(0,signalCounts.JUMP - 1)
				end
			end

			-- Strict mode checks whether the straight replicated movement path
			-- crossed a collidable object. Repeated hits can indicate noclip.
			if strictBaseplateMode and delta.Magnitude >= 4 then
				local pathParams = RaycastParams.new()
				pathParams.FilterType =
					Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				pathParams.FilterDescendantsInstances =
					{selectedCharacter}

				local pathHit = workspace:Raycast(
					lastPosition,
					delta,
					pathParams
				)

				if pathHit
					and pathHit.Instance
					and pathHit.Instance:IsA("BasePart")
					and pathHit.Instance.CanCollide then
					signalCounts.NOCLIP += 1
				else
					signalCounts.NOCLIP =
						math.max(0,signalCounts.NOCLIP - 1)
				end
			end

			local noGround =
				not hasGroundBelow(
					selectedCharacter,
					selectedRoot,
					12
				)

			local humanoidAir =
				selectedHumanoid
				and selectedHumanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air

			if humanoidAir and noGround then
				airborneTime += dt

				-- Hovering: clearly off the ground but barely falling/rising.
				if airborneTime >= (strictBaseplateMode and .75 or 1.35)
					and math.abs(velocity.Y) <= 5 then
					signalCounts.HOVER += 1
					airborneTime = .75
				end

				-- Controlled air travel: moving around while remaining far
				-- above any floor for long enough to rule out a normal jump.
				local horizontalAir =
					Vector3.new(
						velocity.X,
						0,
						velocity.Z
					).Magnitude

				if airborneTime >= (strictBaseplateMode and 1.25 or 2.25)
					and horizontalAir >= 5 then
					signalCounts.AIR += 1
					airborneTime = 1
				end

				-- Repeated/controlled upward travel while there is no nearby
				-- floor. A normal jump may spike once, so require repeats.
				if velocity.Y >= (strictBaseplateMode and 12 or 18) then
					signalCounts.UPWARD += 1
				else
					signalCounts.UPWARD =
						math.max(0,signalCounts.UPWARD - 1)
				end
			else
				airborneTime = 0
				signalCounts.UPWARD =
					math.max(0,signalCounts.UPWARD - 1)
			end
		end

		lastPosition = selectedRoot.Position
		lastSampleTime = now

		local signals = {}
		if signalCounts.SPEED >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"abnormal speed")
		end
		if signalCounts.TELEPORT >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"repeated position jumps")
		end
		if signalCounts.AIR >= 1 then
			table.insert(signals,"controlled airborne travel")
		end
		if signalCounts.HOVER >= 1 then
			table.insert(signals,"hovering with no ground below")
		end
		if signalCounts.UPWARD >= 3 then
			table.insert(signals,"repeated upward flight-like movement")
		end
		if signalCounts.FLING >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"extreme physics / fling-like movement")
		end
		if signalCounts.FORCE >= (strictBaseplateMode and 2 or 5) then
			table.insert(signals,"character force / mover object")
		end
		if signalCounts.JUMP >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"abnormal jump setting")
		end
		if strictBaseplateMode and signalCounts.NOCLIP >= 2 then
			table.insert(signals,"possible noclip through solid parts")
		end

		local serverFlag =
			selectedPlayer:GetAttribute("ExploitDetected") == true
		local serverReason =
			selectedPlayer:GetAttribute("ExploitReason")
		local executorName = normalizeExecutorName(
			selectedPlayer:GetAttribute("ExecutorName")
		)

		local speedOnlyServerFlag = false
		if serverFlag
			and typeof(serverReason) == "string"
			and serverReason ~= "" then
			local loweredReason = string.lower(serverReason)
			speedOnlyServerFlag =
				string.find(loweredReason,"speed",1,true) ~= nil
				and string.find(loweredReason,"flight",1,true) == nil
				and string.find(loweredReason,"air",1,true) == nil
				and string.find(loweredReason,"teleport",1,true) == nil
		end

		if serverFlag and not speedOnlyServerFlag then
			table.insert(signals,"server anti-cheat flag")
		elseif speedOnlyServerFlag then
			table.insert(signals,"fast movement (may be game speed)")
		end

		if typeof(serverReason) == "string"
			and serverReason ~= ""
			and not speedOnlyServerFlag then
			table.insert(signals,"server: "..serverReason)
		end

		local strongLocalSignal =
			signalCounts.TELEPORT >= 2
			or signalCounts.AIR >= 1
			or signalCounts.HOVER >= 1
			or signalCounts.UPWARD >= 3
			or signalCounts.SPEED >= 2
			or signalCounts.FLING >= 2
			or signalCounts.JUMP >= 2
			or (
				strictBaseplateMode
				and (
					signalCounts.FORCE >= 2
					or signalCounts.NOCLIP >= 2
				)
			)

		local suspicious =
			(serverFlag and not speedOnlyServerFlag)
			or executorName ~= nil
			or strongLocalSignal

		if suspicious then
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: SUSPICIOUS"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,92,108)
		else
			verdictLabel.Text = "STATUS: NONE DETECTED"
			verdictLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90,230,135)
		end

		if executorName then
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: "..executorName
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,92,108)
		elseif suspicious then
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: UNKNOWN"
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,170,95)
		else
			executorLabel.Text = "EXECUTOR: NONE"
			executorLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90,230,135)
		end

		if #signals == 0 then
			signalsLabel.Text =
				"SIGNALS: none observed. This detects replicated behavior/character anomalies, not hidden script code. Exact executor names still require server-reported ExecutorName."
		else
			signalsLabel.Text = "SIGNALS: "..table.concat(signals,", ")
		end
	end

	local function selectInspectorPlayer(otherPlayer)
		if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == player then
			return
		end

		selectedPlayer = otherPlayer
		resetInspectorHistory()

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,image = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
					otherPlayer.UserId,
					Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
					Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
				)
			end)
			if ok and image and inspectAvatar.Parent and selectedPlayer == otherPlayer then
				inspectAvatar.Image = image
			end
		end)

		updateInspection()
		setStatus("INSPECTING @"..otherPlayer.Name)
	end

	selectPlayerButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		showPlayerPicker("SELECT PLAYER TO INSPECT",selectInspectorPlayer,false)
	end)

	refreshInspectButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		resetInspectorHistory()
		updateInspection()
		setStatus(selectedPlayer and "INSPECTION REFRESHED" or "SELECT A PLAYER FIRST")
	end)

	clearInspectButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		selectedPlayer = nil
		resetInspectorHistory()
		updateInspection()
		setStatus("INSPECTION CLEARED")
	end)

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)
		if selectedPlayer == leavingPlayer then
			selectedPlayer = nil
			resetInspectorHistory()
			updateInspection()
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while not menuDeleted do
			task.wait(.25)
			if selectedPlayer and currentTab == "EXPLOITER" and exploiterPage.Parent then
				updateInspection()
			end
		end
	end)
end
setupFinalAdminTabs()

local function setToggle(button,on,label)
	if not button or not button.Parent then
		return
	end
	activeToggleButtons[button] = {
		on = on,
		label = label
	}
	if on then
		button.BackgroundColor3 = accent
		button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
		button.Text = label.."  [ON]"
	else
		button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		button.TextColor3 = TEXT
		button.Text = label.."  [OFF]"
	end
end
local function refreshToggleColors()
	for button,data in pairs(activeToggleButtons) do
		if button and button.Parent then
			setToggle(button,data.on,data.label)
		end
	end
end
local function cycleTheme()
	themeIndex += 1
	if themeIndex > #themes then
		themeIndex = 1
	end

	accent = themes[themeIndex].color
	status.TextColor3 = accent
	sidebar.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent

	applyTabVisuals()
	refreshToggleColors()

	-- MATCH makes every outline follow the selected theme.
	if gui:GetAttribute("OutlineMode") == "MATCH" then
		gui:SetAttribute("OutlineColor",accent)
		for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("UIStroke") then
				object.Color = accent
			end
		end
	end

	setStatus("THEME: "..themes[themeIndex].name)
end

)
local function setupHomeProfile()
	local profileCard = Instance.new("Frame")
	profileCard.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,116)
	profileCard.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	profileCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
	profileCard.Parent = homePage
	corner(profileCard,13)

	local profileAvatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	profileAvatar.Name = "HomeProfileAvatar"
	profileAvatar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(84,84)
	profileAvatar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14,16)
	profileAvatar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	profileAvatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	profileAvatar.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
	profileAvatar.Parent = profileCard
	corner(profileAvatar,42)
	addStroke(profileAvatar,accent,1.5,.1)

	local profileName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	profileName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	profileName.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(112,18)
	profileName.Size = UDim2.new(1,-126,0,24)
	profileName.Text = player.DisplayName
	profileName.TextSize = 16
	profileName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	profileName.TextColor3 = TEXT
	profileName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	profileName.Parent = profileCard

	local profileUser = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	profileUser.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	profileUser.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(112,44)
	profileUser.Size = UDim2.new(1,-126,0,18)
	profileUser.Text = "@"..player.Name
	profileUser.TextSize = 10
	profileUser.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	profileUser.TextColor3 = MUTED
	profileUser.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	profileUser.Parent = profileCard

	local profileInfo = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	profileInfo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	profileInfo.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(112,66)
	profileInfo.Size = UDim2.new(1,-126,0,36)
	profileInfo.Text =
		"USER ID: "..tostring(player.UserId)
		.."\nACCOUNT AGE: "..tostring(player.AccountAge).." DAYS"
	profileInfo.TextSize = 9
	profileInfo.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	profileInfo.TextColor3 = MUTED
	profileInfo.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	profileInfo.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	profileInfo.Parent = profileCard

	task.spawn(function()
		local ok,image = pcall(function()
			return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
				player.UserId,
				Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust,
				Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420
			)
		end)
		if ok and image and profileAvatar.Parent then
			profileAvatar.Image = image
		end
	end)
end
