				hudScale.Scale = .86
			end
			hud.Visible = true
			if hudScale then
				TweenService:Create(
					hudScale,
					TweenInfo.new(.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = 1}
				):Play()
			end
		else
			if hudScale then
				local tween = TweenService:Create(
					hudScale,
					TweenInfo.new(.1,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.In),
					{Scale = .88}
				)
				tween:Play()
				tween.Completed:Once(function()
					if not liveHudEnabled and hud.Parent then
						hud.Visible = false
						hudScale.Scale = 1
					end
				end)
			else
				hud.Visible = false
			end
		end

		setToggle(hudButton,liveHudEnabled,"LIVE HUD")
		setStatus(liveHudEnabled and "LIVE HUD ON" or "LIVE HUD OFF")
	end)
	local function releaseFakeLagRoot()
		if fakeLagHeldRoot and fakeLagHeldRoot.Parent then
			-- Do not fight the normal Freeze option if it was enabled separately.
			if not frozen then
				fakeLagHeldRoot.Anchored = false
			end
		end
		fakeLagHeldRoot = nil
	end

	local function stopFakeLag()
		fakeLagEnabled = false
		gui:SetAttribute("FakeLagEnabled",false)
		fakeLagToken += 1
		releaseFakeLagRoot()
	end

	local function startFakeLag()
		stopFakeLag()

		-- Fake Lag and the permanent Freeze button both use Anchored,
		-- so turn Freeze off first to avoid the two systems fighting.
		if frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
			frozenRoot = nil
		end
		frozen = false
		setToggle(freezeButton,false,"FREEZE")

		fakeLagEnabled = true
		fakeLagToken += 1
		local token = fakeLagToken

		task.spawn(function()
			while fakeLagEnabled and fakeLagToken == token do
				if not root or not root.Parent then
					task.wait(.1)
					continue
				end

				local currentRoot = root
				fakeLagHeldRoot = currentRoot
				currentRoot.Anchored = true

				local elapsed = 0
				while fakeLagEnabled
					and fakeLagToken == token
					and currentRoot.Parent
					and elapsed < fakeLagHold do

					local step = math.min(.05,fakeLagHold - elapsed)
					task.wait(step)
					elapsed += step
				end

				if currentRoot.Parent and currentRoot.Anchored and not frozen then
					currentRoot.Anchored = false
				end
				fakeLagHeldRoot = nil

				if fakeLagEnabled and fakeLagToken == token then
					task.wait(fakeLagRelease)
				end
			end

			releaseFakeLagRoot()
		end)
	end

	local function setFakeLag(value)
		gui:SetAttribute("FakeLagEnabled",value == true)
		if value then
			startFakeLag()
		else
			stopFakeLag()
		end

		setToggle(fakeLagButton,fakeLagEnabled,"FAKE LAG")
		setStatus(fakeLagEnabled and "FAKE LAG ON" or "FAKE LAG OFF")
	end

	fakeLagApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(fakeLagBox.Text)
		if not value then
			setStatus("INVALID FAKE LAG STRENGTH / FREEZE")
			return
		end

		fakeLagHold = math.clamp(value,.05,10)
		fakeLagBox.Text = tostring(fakeLagHold)
		setStatus("FAKE LAG STRENGTH / FREEZE "..tostring(fakeLagHold).."s")
	end)

	fakeLagButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setFakeLag(not fakeLagEnabled)
	end)

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("CommandInvisible"):Connect(function()
		setInvisible(not invisibleEnabled)
	end)

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal("CommandFakeLag"):Connect(function()
		setFakeLag(not fakeLagEnabled)
	end)
	freezeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not root or not root.Parent then
			loadCharacter()
		end
		frozen = not frozen
		if frozen and root then
			frozenRoot = root
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			root.Anchored = true
		elseif frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
			frozenRoot = nil
		end
		setToggle(freezeButton,frozen,"FREEZE")
		setStatus(frozen and "CHARACTER FROZEN" or "CHARACTER UNFROZEN")
	end)
	sitButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if humanoid then
			humanoid.Sit = not humanoid.Sit
			setStatus(humanoid.Sit and "SITTING" or "STANDING")
		end
	end)
	jumpButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not character or not character.Parent or not humanoid or not root or not root.Parent then
			loadCharacter()
		end
		if frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
			frozenRoot = nil
			frozen = false
			setToggle(freezeButton,false,"FREEZE")
		end
		if humanoid and root then
			if fakeLagEnabled and root.Anchored then
				root.Anchored = false
				fakeLagHeldRoot = nil
				task.wait()
			end
			humanoid.Sit = false
			humanoid.Jump = true
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			end)
			local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
				velocity.X,
				math.max(velocity.Y,jumpPower),
				velocity.Z
			)
			setStatus("JUMP")
		end
	end)
	local function isPerformanceObject(object)
		return object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
			or object:IsA("Trail")
			or object:IsA("Beam")
			or object:IsA("Smoke")
			or object:IsA("Fire")
			or object:IsA("Sparkles")
	end
	local function disablePerformanceObject(object)
		if not performanceMode or not isPerformanceObject(object) then
			return
		end
		if performanceStates[object] == nil then
			performanceStates[object] = object.Enabled
		end
		object.Enabled = false
	end
	local originalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
	local function restorePerformance()
		for object,enabled in pairs(performanceStates) do
			if object and object.Parent then
				object.Enabled = enabled
			end
		end
		table.clear(performanceStates)
		Lighting.GlobalShadows = originalShadows
	end
	local function setPerformance(value)
		performanceMode = value
		if performanceMode then
			originalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
			Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
			for _,object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				disablePerformanceObject(object)
			end
		else
			restorePerformance()
		end
		setToggle(performanceButton,performanceMode,"PERFORMANCE")
		setStatus(performanceMode and "PERFORMANCE MODE ON" or "PERFORMANCE MODE OFF")
	end
	workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
		if menuDeleted or not performanceMode then
			return
		end
		task.defer(function()
			if not menuDeleted then
				disablePerformanceObject(object)
			end
		end)
	end)
	performanceButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setPerformance(not performanceMode)
	end)
	cameraResetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera and humanoid then
			camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
			camera.FieldOfView = originalFOV
			fovBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(originalFOV))
			setStatus("CAMERA RESET")
		end
	end)
	refreshCharacterButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		local oldCharacter = player.Character
		local wasFlying = flying

		if frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
			frozenRoot = nil
			frozen = false
			setToggle(freezeButton,false,"FREEZE")
		end

		if wasFlying then
			stopFlight()
		end

		setStatus("REFRESHING CHARACTER...")

		local oldHumanoid =
			oldCharacter
			and oldCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if oldHumanoid then
			oldHumanoid.Health = 0
		elseif oldCharacter then
			pcall(function()
				oldCharacter:BreakJoints()
			end)
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local deadline = os.clock() + 10

			repeat
				task.wait(.1)
			until menuDeleted
				or (
					player.Character
					and player.Character ~= oldCharacter
				)
				or os.clock() >= deadline

			if menuDeleted then
				return
			end

			if not player.Character or player.Character == oldCharacter then
				setStatus("CHARACTER REFRESH FAILED")
				return
			end

			loadCharacter()
			task.wait(.15)
			applyMovementStats()

			if wasFlying and root and humanoid then
				startFlight()
			end

			if playerLocator then
				refreshPlayers()
			end

			if monsterLocator then
				task.defer(refreshMonsters)
			end

			setStatus("CHARACTER REFRESHED")
		end)
	end)
	respawnButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if humanoid then
			humanoid.Health = 0
			setStatus("RESPAWNING")
		end
	end)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		antiFlingLastSafeCFrame = nil
		restoreAntiFlingCollisions()
		frozen = false
		frozenRoot = nil
		fakeLagHeldRoot = nil
		if freezeButton and freezeButton.Parent then
			setToggle(freezeButton,false,"FREEZE")
		end
	end)
	universalCleanup = function()
		liveHudEnabled = false
		if hud and hud.Parent then
			hud.Visible = false
		end
		if frozenRoot and frozenRoot.Parent then
			frozenRoot.Anchored = false
		end
		frozen = false
		frozenRoot = nil
		if performanceMode then
			performanceMode = false
			restorePerformance()
		end
		if antiFlingEnabled then
			stopAntiFling()
		else
			restoreAntiFlingCollisions()
		end
		if fakeLagEnabled then
			stopFakeLag()
		else
			releaseFakeLagRoot()
		end
		if fakeLagButton and fakeLagButton.Parent then
			setToggle(fakeLagButton,false,"FAKE LAG")
		end
		if invisibleEnabled then
			invisibleEnabled = false
			restoreInvisible()
		end
		if invisibleButton and invisibleButton.Parent then
			setToggle(invisibleButton,false,"INVISIBLE")
		end
		workspace.Gravity = originalGravity
	end
end
setupUniversalExtras()
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(otherPlayer)
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if not playerLocator then
		return
	end
	otherPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(.2)
		if playerLocator then
			refreshPlayers()
		end
	end)
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	if playerLocator then
		task.defer(refreshPlayers)
	end
end)
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
	if menuDeleted then
		return
	end
	stopFlight()
	restoreCollision()
	character = newCharacter
	humanoid = newCharacter:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	root = newCharacter:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	task.wait(.1)
	applyMovementStats()
	if playerLocator then
		refreshPlayers()
	end
	if monsterLocator then
		task.defer(refreshMonsters)
	end
end)

local function setupPowerSuite()
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

	local state = {
		file = "not_ruby_v4_power_"..tostring(player.UserId)..".json",
		canFiles =
			type(isfile) == "function"
			and type(readfile) == "function"
			and type(writefile) == "function",
		data = {
			favorites = {},
			keybinds = {
				Flight = "F",
				Noclip = "N",
				Invisible = "X",
				FakeLag = "L",
				WallParts = "V"
			},
			waypoints = {},
			presets = {},
			settings = {}
		},
		registry = {},
		waypointObjects = {},
		stats = {
			started = os.clock(),
			distance = 0,
			maxSpeed = 0,
			jumps = 0,
			fps = 0,
			minFps = 999,
			lastPosition = nil
		}
	}

	local ui = {}

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Storage
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local function loadStorage()
		if not state.canFiles then
			return
		end

		pcall(function()
			if not isfile(state.file) then
				return
			end

			local decoded =
				HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(state.file))

			if typeof(decoded) ~= "table" then
				return
			end

			for key,value in pairs(decoded) do
				state.data[key] = value
			end

			state.data.favorites =
				typeof(state.data.favorites) == "table"
				and state.data.favorites
				or {}
			state.data.keybinds =
				typeof(state.data.keybinds) == "table"
				and state.data.keybinds
				or {}
			state.data.waypoints =
				typeof(state.data.waypoints) == "table"
				and state.data.waypoints
				or {}
			state.data.presets =
				typeof(state.data.presets) == "table"
				and state.data.presets
				or {}
			state.data.settings =
				typeof(state.data.settings) == "table"
				and state.data.settings
				or {}

			local defaults = {
				Flight = "F",
				Noclip = "N",
				Invisible = "X",
				FakeLag = "L",
				WallParts = "V"
			}

			for feature,keyName in pairs(defaults) do
				if type(state.data.keybinds[feature]) ~= "string" then
					state.data.keybinds[feature] = keyName
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function saveStorage()
		if not state.canFiles then
			return false
		end

		local ok = pcall(function()
			writefile(
				state.file,
				HttpService:JSONEncode(state.data)
			)
		end)

		return ok
	end

	loadStorage()

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Error notification sound system
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local errorSoundOptions = {
		{name="RUBY ALERT",id="85552330273422"},
		{name="VOID PULSE",id="79908084264003"},
		{name="SYSTEM GLITCH",id="134254842665299"},
		{name="CRITICAL SIGNAL",id="91681144991126"},
		{name="NEON WARNING",id="131447940085093"},
		{name="EXECUTOR ALERT",id="92466756400564"},

		-- Public notification sounds found in Roblox Creator Store.
		{name="QUICK NOTIFICATION",id="7383525713"},
		{name="SHORT NOTICE",id="18595195017"},
		{name="NOTIFICATION BEEP",id="6176999962"}
	}

	local errorSoundEnabled =
		state.data.settings.errorSoundEnabled ~= false
	local errorSoundIndex =
		math.clamp(
			math.floor(
				tonumber(state.data.settings.errorSoundIndex) or 1
			),
			1,
			#errorSoundOptions
		)

	local customErrorSoundId =
		tostring(state.data.settings.customErrorSoundId or "")
			:gsub("%D","")

	local useCustomErrorSound =
		state.data.settings.useCustomErrorSound == true
		and customErrorSoundId ~= ""

	local function currentErrorSoundId()
		if useCustomErrorSound and customErrorSoundId ~= "" then
			return customErrorSoundId
		end

		return errorSoundOptions[errorSoundIndex].id
	end

	local function currentErrorSoundName()
		if useCustomErrorSound and customErrorSoundId ~= "" then
			return "CUSTOM "..customErrorSoundId
		end

		return errorSoundOptions[errorSoundIndex].name
	end

	local errorSoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
	local errorContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
	local errorSound = Instance.new("Sound")
	errorSound.Name = "NotRubyErrorSound"
	errorSound.Volume = 1
	errorSound.PlaybackSpeed = 1
	errorSound.SoundId =
		"rbxassetid://"..currentErrorSoundId()
	errorSound.Parent = errorSoundService
	local lastErrorSoundAt = 0
	local lastErrorToastText = ""
	local lastErrorToastAt = 0

	gui:SetAttribute("ErrorSoundEnabled",errorSoundEnabled)
	gui:SetAttribute(
		"ErrorSoundId",
		currentErrorSoundId()
	)

	local function playErrorSound(forcePreview)
		if not forcePreview and not errorSoundEnabled then
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		if not forcePreview and now-lastErrorSoundAt < .65 then
			return
		end
		lastErrorSoundAt = now

		errorSound.SoundId =
			"rbxassetid://"..currentErrorSoundId()

		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(function()
				errorContentProvider:PreloadAsync({errorSound})
			end)

			if not errorSound or not errorSound.Parent then
				return
			end

			pcall(function()
				errorSound:Stop()
				errorSound.TimePosition = 0
			end)

			local played =
				pcall(function()
					errorSoundService:PlayLocalSound(errorSound)
				end)

			if not played then
				pcall(function()
					errorSound:Play()
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function isErrorNotification(message)
		local upper = string.upper(tostring(message or ""))

		local patterns = {
			"INVALID",
			"FAILED",
			"UNAVAILABLE",
			"NOT FOUND",
			"NO VALID TARGET",
			"NO PLAYER",
			"NO TARGET",
			"NO WEAPON",
			"NO MODEL",
			"NO CHARACTER",
			"PATH BLOCKED",
			"TARGET BLOCKED",
			"SERVER DENIED",
			"SERVER COMPONENT MISSING",
			"SERVER FLING ERROR",
			"FLING ERROR",
			"AIMBOT ERROR",
			"AIM ASSIST ERROR",
			"AUTO SHOOT ERROR",
			"AUTO DODGE ERROR",
			"PRESET SLOT IS EMPTY",
			"FILE STORAGE UNAVAILABLE",
			"CLIPBOARD UNAVAILABLE",
			"COPY FAILED",
			"LANGUAGE NOT FOUND",
			"NO FEATURE FOUND",
			"NO COUNTRY FOUND",
			"SELECT A PLAYER FIRST",
			"SELECT A TARGET FIRST",
			"INCORRECT PASSWORD",
			"LOCATOR IS OFF",
			"SET AIM_ASSIST_PLACE_ID"
		}

		for _,pattern in ipairs(patterns) do
			if string.find(upper,pattern,1,true) then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Animated notification system
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local notifyEvent = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyNotifyEvent")
	if not notifyEvent then
		notifyEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		notifyEvent.Name = "NotRubyNotifyEvent"
		notifyEvent.Parent = gui
	end

	ui.toastHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
	ui.toastHolder.Name = "NotRubyToasts"
	ui.toastHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
	ui.toastHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1,-12,0,12)
	ui.toastHolder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300,360)
	ui.toastHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ui.toastHolder.ZIndex = 1200
	ui.toastHolder.Parent = gui

	local toastLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	toastLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,6)
	toastLayout.HorizontalAlignment =
		Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
	toastLayout.VerticalAlignment =
		Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
	toastLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	toastLayout.Parent = ui.toastHolder

	local function notify(message)
		if menuDeleted or not ui.toastHolder.Parent then
			return
		end

		message = tostring(message or "")
		local errorMode = isErrorNotification(message)
		local now = os.clock()

		-- Repeated frame-level failures should not flood the screen or audio.
		if errorMode
			and message == lastErrorToastText
			and now-lastErrorToastAt < 1.15 then
			return
		end

		if errorMode then
			lastErrorToastText = message
			lastErrorToastAt = now
			playErrorSound(false)
		end

		local toast = Instance.new("Frame")
		toast.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(286,48)
		toast.BackgroundColor3 =
			errorMode
			and Color3.fromRGB(68,18,28)
			or PANEL
		toast.BackgroundTransparency = .04
		toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
		toast.ZIndex = 1201
		toast.Parent = ui.toastHolder
		corner(toast,11)
		addStroke(
			toast,
			errorMode
			and Color3.fromRGB(255,75,96)
			or (gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent),
			errorMode and 1.7 or 1.2,
			.04
		)

		local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		scale.Scale = .82
		scale.Parent = toast

		if errorMode then
			local errorMark = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			errorMark.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			errorMark.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9,5)
			errorMark.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24,38)
			errorMark.Text = "!"
			errorMark.TextSize = 17
			errorMark.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
			errorMark.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,92,108)
			errorMark.ZIndex = 1202
			errorMark.Parent = toast
		end

		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Position =
			errorMode
			and UDim2.fromOffset(34,6)
			or UDim2.fromOffset(11,6)
		label.Size =
			errorMode
			and UDim2.new(1,-44,1,-12)
			or UDim2.new(1,-22,1,-12)
		label.Text = message
		label.TextSize = 9
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		label.TextColor3 = TEXT
		label.TextWrapped = true
		label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		label.ZIndex = 1202
		label.Parent = toast

		TweenService:Create(
			scale,
			TweenInfo.new(
				.16,
				Enum.EasingStyle.Back,
				Enum.EasingDirection.Out
			),
			{Scale = 1}
		):Play()

		task.delay(errorMode and 3.2 or 2.6,function()
			if not toast.Parent then
				return
			end

			TweenService:Create(
				scale,
				TweenInfo.new(.14),
				{Scale = .82}
			):Play()
			TweenService:Create(
				toast,
				TweenInfo.new(.14),
				{BackgroundTransparency = 1}
			):Play()

			task.wait(.15)
			if toast.Parent then
				toast:Destroy()
			end
		end)
	end

	notifyEvent.Event:Connect(notify)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Utility tabs
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	ui.favoritesPage = makePage("FAVORITES")
	ui.waypointsPage = makePage("WAYPOINTS")
	ui.statsPage = makePage("STATS")

	makeTab("FAVORITES","FAVORITES").Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("FAVORITES")
	end)
	makeTab("WAYPOINTS","WAYPOINTS").Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("WAYPOINTS")
	end)
	makeTab("STATS","SESSION STATS").Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("STATS")
	end)

	-- FEATURE SEARCH now lives permanently above the content area,
	-- instead of taking up a sidebar tab.
	content.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(140,104)
	content.Size = UDim2.new(1,-147,1,-111)

	ui.searchBar = Instance.new("Frame")
	ui.searchBar.Name = "TopFeatureSearch"
	ui.searchBar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(140,59)
	ui.searchBar.Size = UDim2.new(1,-147,0,38)
	ui.searchBar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	ui.searchBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.searchBar.ZIndex = 240
	ui.searchBar.Parent = panel
	corner(ui.searchBar,10)
	addStroke(
		ui.searchBar,
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.1,
		.1
	)

	local searchPrefix = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	searchPrefix.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	searchPrefix.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9,0)
	searchPrefix.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48,38)
	searchPrefix.Text = "SEARCH"
	searchPrefix.TextSize = 8
	searchPrefix.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	searchPrefix.TextColor3 = accent
	searchPrefix.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	searchPrefix.ZIndex = 241
	searchPrefix.Parent = ui.searchBar

	ui.searchBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
	ui.searchBox.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(55,4)
	ui.searchBox.Size = UDim2.new(1,-63,0,30)
	ui.searchBox.Text = ""
	ui.searchBox.PlaceholderText =
		"Flight, invisible, wall parts, theme..."
	ui.searchBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	ui.searchBox.TextSize = 9
	ui.searchBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	ui.searchBox.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.searchBox.PlaceholderColor3 = MUTED
	ui.searchBox.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.searchBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.searchBox.ZIndex = 241
	ui.searchBox.Parent = ui.searchBar
	corner(ui.searchBox,8)

	ui.searchResults = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	ui.searchResults.Name = "TopFeatureSearchResults"
	ui.searchResults.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(140,101)
	ui.searchResults.Size = UDim2.new(1,-147,0,0)
	ui.searchResults.BackgroundColor3 = PANEL
	ui.searchResults.BackgroundTransparency = .02
	ui.searchResults.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.searchResults.ScrollBarThickness = 3
	ui.searchResults.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
	ui.searchResults.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
	ui.searchResults.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	ui.searchResults.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
	ui.searchResults.ClipsDescendants = true
	ui.searchResults.Visible = false
	ui.searchResults.ZIndex = 250
	ui.searchResults.Parent = panel
	corner(ui.searchResults,10)
	addStroke(
		ui.searchResults,
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.2,
		.04
	)

	local searchLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	searchLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,5)
	searchLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	searchLayout.Parent = ui.searchResults

	local searchPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	searchPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,5)
	searchPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0,5)
	searchPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,5)
	searchPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,5)
	searchPadding.Parent = ui.searchResults

	makeSection(
		ui.favoritesPage,
		"FAVORITES",
		"Tap the star on any feature button. Favorites appear here for quick navigation."
	)

	ui.favoriteResults = Instance.new("Frame")
	ui.favoriteResults.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
	ui.favoriteResults.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	ui.favoriteResults.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ui.favoriteResults.Parent = ui.favoritesPage

	local favoriteLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	favoriteLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,6)
	favoriteLayout.Parent = ui.favoriteResults

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Search + Favorites
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local function cleanLabel(value)
		value = tostring(value or "")
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+%[ON%]","")
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+%[OFF%]","")
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+"," ")
		return value
	end

	local function pageForObject(object)
		for pageName,page in pairs(pages) do
			if object:IsDescendantOf(page) then
				return pageName,page
			end
		end
		return nil,nil
	end

	local function jumpToFeature(entry)
		if not entry
			or not entry.button
			or not entry.button.Parent then
			return
		end

		local page = pages[entry.page]
		if not page then
			return
		end

		setTab(entry.page)

		task.defer(function()
			task.wait()
			local relative =
				entry.button.AbsolutePosition.Y
				- page.AbsolutePosition.Y
				+ page.CanvasPosition.Y
				- 55

			page.CanvasPosition =
				Vector2.new(0,math.max(0,relative))

			local scale =
				entry.button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")

			if scale then
				scale.Scale = .92
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(
						.22,
						Enum.EasingStyle.Back,
						Enum.EasingDirection.Out
					),
					{Scale = 1}
				):Play()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function clearChildren(frame)
		for _,child in ipairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
			if not child:IsA("UIListLayout")
				and not child:IsA("UIPadding") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	local rebuildFavorites

	local function makeFavoriteStar(button,pageName)
		if button:GetAttribute("NotRubyFavoriteStarred") then
			return
		end

		if button.Name == "FavoriteStar"
			or button.Text == ""
			or button:IsDescendantOf(ui.favoritesPage)
			or button:IsDescendantOf(ui.waypointsPage)
			or button:IsDescendantOf(ui.statsPage) then
			return
		end

		local label = cleanLabel(button.Text)
		if label == "" then
			return
		end

		local key = pageName.."|"..label

		button:SetAttribute("NotRubyFavoriteStarred",true)
		button:SetAttribute("NotRubyFeatureKey",key)

		state.registry[key] = {
			button = button,
			page = pageName,
			label = label
		}

		local star = Instance.new("TextButton")
		star.Name = "FavoriteStar"
		star.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
		star.Position = UDim2.new(1,-3,0,3)
		star.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(21,21)
		star.Text =
			state.data.favorites[key]
			and "★"
			or "☆"
		star.TextSize = 13
		star.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		star.TextColor3 = accent
		star.BackgroundColor3 = PANEL
		star.BackgroundTransparency = .14
		star.BorderSizePixel = 0
		star.AutoButtonColor = false
		star.ZIndex = button.ZIndex + 10
		star.Parent = button
		corner(star,7)

		star.Activated:Connect(function()
			if state.data.favorites[key] then
				state.data.favorites[key] = nil
				star.Text = "☆"
				notify("REMOVED FROM FAVORITES // "..label)
			else
				state.data.favorites[key] = true
				star.Text = "★"
				notify("ADDED TO FAVORITES // "..label)
			end

			saveStorage()
			if rebuildFavorites then
				rebuildFavorites()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function scanFeatureButtons()
		local excluded = {
			FAVORITES = true,
			WAYPOINTS = true,
			STATS = true
		}

		for pageName,page in pairs(pages) do
			if not excluded[pageName] then
				for _,object in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
					if object:IsA("TextButton")
						and object.Name ~= "FavoriteStar"
						and not object.Parent:IsA("TextButton") then
						makeFavoriteStar(object,pageName)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	rebuildFavorites = function()
		clearChildren(ui.favoriteResults)

		local count = 0
		for key,enabled in pairs(state.data.favorites) do
			local entry = state.registry[key]

			if enabled and entry
				and entry.button
				and entry.button.Parent then

				count += 1
				local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
				button.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42)
				button.Text =
					"★  ["
					..entry.page
					.."]  "
					..entry.label
				button.TextSize = 9
				button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				button.TextColor3 = TEXT
				button.TextXAlignment =
					Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				button.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
				button.BorderSizePixel = 0
				button.AutoButtonColor = false
				button.Parent = ui.favoriteResults
				corner(button,10)

				local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
				padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,12)
				padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,8)
				padding.Parent = button

				button.Activated:Connect(function()
					jumpToFeature(entry)
				end)
			end
		end

		if count == 0 then
			local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,50)
			empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			empty.Text =
				"NO FAVORITES YET // TAP ☆ ON A FEATURE"
			empty.TextSize = 9
			empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			empty.TextColor3 = MUTED
			empty.Parent = ui.favoriteResults
		end
	end

	local function rebuildSearch()
		clearChildren(ui.searchResults)

		local query =
			string.lower(
				string.gsub(
					ui.searchBox.Text or "",
					"^%s*(.-)%s*$",
					"%1"
				)
			)

		if query == "" then
			ui.searchResults.Visible = false
			ui.searchResults.Size = UDim2.new(1,-147,0,0)
			return
		end

		local matches = {}

		for _,entry in pairs(state.registry) do
			local haystack =
				string.lower(
					entry.label.." "..entry.page
				)

			if string.find(haystack,query,1,true) then
				table.insert(matches,entry)
			end
		end

		table.sort(matches,function(a,b)
			return a.label < b.label
		end)

		local shown = math.min(#matches,6)

		ui.searchResults.Visible = true
		ui.searchResults.Size =
			UDim2.new(
				1,
				-147,
				0,
				shown > 0 and (shown*41 + 10) or 54
			)

		for index,entry in ipairs(matches) do
			if index > 24 then
				break
			end

			local result = Instance.new("TextButton")
			result.Size = UDim2.new(1,-10,0,36)
			result.Text =
				"["..entry.page.."]  "..entry.label
			result.TextSize = 9
			result.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			result.TextColor3 = TEXT
			result.TextXAlignment =
				Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			result.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
			result.BorderSizePixel = 0
			result.AutoButtonColor = false
			result.LayoutOrder = index
			result.ZIndex = 251
			result.Parent = ui.searchResults
			corner(result,9)

			local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
			padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,11)
			padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,8)
			padding.Parent = result

			result.Activated:Connect(function()
				ui.searchBox.Text = ""
				ui.searchResults.Visible = false
				jumpToFeature(entry)
			end)
		end

		if #matches == 0 then
			local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,-10,0,40)
			empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			empty.Text = "NO FEATURE FOUND"
			empty.TextSize = 9
			empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			empty.TextColor3 = MUTED
			empty.ZIndex = 251
			empty.Parent = ui.searchResults
		end
	end

	ui.searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(
		rebuildSearch
	)

	ui.searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		if ui.searchBox.Text == "" then
			ui.searchResults.Visible = false
		end
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Waypoints
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		ui.waypointsPage,
		"WAYPOINTS",
		"Save named positions and keep a live marker/distance on screen. This does not teleport you."
	)

	ui.waypointName = Instance.new("TextBox")
	ui.waypointName.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,40)
	ui.waypointName.Text = ""
	ui.waypointName.PlaceholderText = "Waypoint name"
	ui.waypointName.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	ui.waypointName.TextSize = 10
	ui.waypointName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	ui.waypointName.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.waypointName.PlaceholderColor3 = MUTED
	ui.waypointName.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.waypointName.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.waypointName.Parent = ui.waypointsPage
	corner(ui.waypointName,10)

	local waypointActions = Instance.new("Frame")
	waypointActions.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42)
	waypointActions.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	waypointActions.Parent = ui.waypointsPage

	ui.saveWaypoint = Instance.new("TextButton")
	ui.saveWaypoint.Size = UDim2.new(.6,-4,1,0)
	ui.saveWaypoint.Text = "SAVE CURRENT POSITION"
	ui.saveWaypoint.TextSize = 9
	ui.saveWaypoint.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	ui.saveWaypoint.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.saveWaypoint.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.saveWaypoint.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.saveWaypoint.AutoButtonColor = false
	ui.saveWaypoint.Parent = waypointActions
	corner(ui.saveWaypoint,10)

	ui.clearWaypoints = Instance.new("TextButton")
	ui.clearWaypoints.Position = UDim2.new(.6,4,0,0)
	ui.clearWaypoints.Size = UDim2.new(.4,-4,1,0)
	ui.clearWaypoints.Text = "CLEAR ALL"
	ui.clearWaypoints.TextSize = 9
	ui.clearWaypoints.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	ui.clearWaypoints.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.clearWaypoints.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.clearWaypoints.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.clearWaypoints.AutoButtonColor = false
	ui.clearWaypoints.Parent = waypointActions
	corner(ui.clearWaypoints,10)

	ui.waypointList = Instance.new("Frame")
	ui.waypointList.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
	ui.waypointList.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	ui.waypointList.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	ui.waypointList.Parent = ui.waypointsPage

	local waypointLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	waypointLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,6)
	waypointLayout.Parent = ui.waypointList

	ui.waypointFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	ui.waypointFolder.Name = "NotRubyWaypoints"
	ui.waypointFolder.Parent = workspace

	local rebuildWaypoints

	local function destroyWaypointObjects()
		for _,object in pairs(state.waypointObjects) do
			if object and object.Parent then
				object:Destroy()
			end
		end
		table.clear(state.waypointObjects)
	end

	local function makeWaypointObject(index,waypoint)
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Name = "Waypoint_"..tostring(index)
		part.Size = Vector3.new(.2,.2,.2)
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.CanTouch = false
		part.CanQuery = false
		part.Transparency = 1
		part.Position = Vector3.new(
			tonumber(waypoint.x) or 0,
			tonumber(waypoint.y) or 0,
			tonumber(waypoint.z) or 0
		)
		part.Parent = ui.waypointFolder

		local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		billboard.Name = "WaypointBillboard"
		billboard.Adornee = part
		billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220,48)
		billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,2.5,0)
		billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboard.Parent = part

		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Name = "Label"
		label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Text =
			"◆ "
			..tostring(waypoint.name or ("Waypoint "..index))
		label.TextSize = 10
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		label.TextColor3 = accent
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = .25
		label.Parent = billboard

		state.waypointObjects[index] = part
	end

	rebuildWaypoints = function()
		clearChildren(ui.waypointList)
		destroyWaypointObjects()

		for index,waypoint in ipairs(state.data.waypoints) do
			makeWaypointObject(index,waypoint)

			local row = Instance.new("Frame")
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,48)
			row.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
			row.BorderSizePixel = 0
			row.Parent = ui.waypointList
			corner(row,10)

			local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,5)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-92,1,-10)
			label.Text =
				tostring(waypoint.name or ("Waypoint "..index))
				.."  //  "
				..math.floor(tonumber(waypoint.x) or 0)
				..", "
				..math.floor(tonumber(waypoint.y) or 0)
				..", "
				..math.floor(tonumber(waypoint.z) or 0)
			label.TextSize = 8
			label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			label.TextColor3 = TEXT
			label.TextWrapped = true
			label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			label.Parent = row

			local deleteButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
			deleteButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,.5)
			deleteButton.Position = UDim2.new(1,-7,.5,0)
			deleteButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72,32)
			deleteButton.Text = "DELETE"
			deleteButton.TextSize = 8
			deleteButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
			deleteButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
			deleteButton.BackgroundColor3 =
				Color3.fromRGB(92,32,43)
			deleteButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
			deleteButton.AutoButtonColor = false
			deleteButton.Parent = row
			corner(deleteButton,8)

			deleteButton.Activated:Connect(function()
				table.remove(state.data.waypoints,index)
				saveStorage()
				rebuildWaypoints()
				notify("WAYPOINT DELETED")
			end)
		end

		if #state.data.waypoints == 0 then
			local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
			empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			empty.Text = "NO WAYPOINTS SAVED"
			empty.TextSize = 9
			empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			empty.TextColor3 = MUTED
			empty.Parent = ui.waypointList
		end
	end

	ui.saveWaypoint.Activated:Connect(function()
		local currentRoot =
			player.Character
			and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not currentRoot then
			notify("NO CHARACTER ROOT FOUND")
			return
		end

		if #state.data.waypoints >= 30 then
			notify("WAYPOINT LIMIT REACHED // 30")
			return
		end

		local name =
			string.gsub(
				ui.waypointName.Text or "",
				"^%s*(.-)%s*$",
				"%1"
			)

		if name == "" then
			name =
				"WAYPOINT "
				..tostring(#state.data.waypoints + 1)
		end

		table.insert(
			state.data.waypoints,
			{
				name = name,
				x = currentRoot.Position.X,
				y = currentRoot.Position.Y,
				z = currentRoot.Position.Z
			}
		)

		ui.waypointName.Text = ""
		saveStorage()
		rebuildWaypoints()
		notify("WAYPOINT SAVED // "..name)
	end)

	ui.clearWaypoints.Activated:Connect(function()
		table.clear(state.data.waypoints)
		saveStorage()
		rebuildWaypoints()
		notify("ALL WAYPOINTS CLEARED")
	end)

	rebuildWaypoints()

	-- Live distance on waypoint markers.
	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			local currentRoot =
				player.Character
				and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if currentRoot then
				for index,part in pairs(state.waypointObjects) do
					local waypoint = state.data.waypoints[index]
					local billboard =
						part
						and part:FindFirstChild("WaypointBillboard")
					local label =
						billboard
						and billboard:FindFirstChild("Label")

					if waypoint and label then
						local distance =
							math.floor(
								(currentRoot.Position-part.Position)
								.Magnitude
							)

						label.Text =
							"◆ "
							..tostring(waypoint.name)
							.." // "
							..tostring(distance)
							.." studs"
						label.TextColor3 = accent
					end
				end
			end

			task.wait(.35)
		end
	end)

	makeSection(

		"Live session information: time, distance, speed, jumps and FPS."
	)

	local function makeStatRow(titleText)
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,44)
		frame.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
		frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame.Parent = ui.statsPage
		corner(frame,10)

		local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11,0)
		title.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-12,1,0)
		title.Text = titleText
		title.TextSize = 9
		title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		title.TextColor3 = MUTED
		title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		title.Parent = frame

		local value = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		value.Position = UDim2.new(.5,0,0,0)
		value.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-11,1,0)
		value.Text = "--"
		value.TextSize = 10
		value.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		value.TextColor3 = TEXT
		value.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		value.Parent = frame

		return value
	end

	ui.statTime = makeStatRow("TIME PLAYED")
	ui.statDistance = makeStatRow("DISTANCE MOVED")
	ui.statSpeed = makeStatRow("CURRENT / MAX SPEED")
	ui.statJumps = makeStatRow("JUMPS")
	ui.statFps = makeStatRow("FPS / MIN FPS")
	ui.statPlayers = makeStatRow("PLAYERS")

	local fpsFrames = 0
	local fpsStarted = os.clock()
	local humanoidConnection

	local function connectStatsHumanoid()
		if humanoidConnection then
			humanoidConnection:Disconnect()
			humanoidConnection = nil
		end

		local currentHumanoid =
			player.Character
			and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if currentHumanoid then
			humanoidConnection =
				currentHumanoid.StateChanged:Connect(
					function(_,newState)
						if newState
							== Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
							state.stats.jumps += 1
						end
					end
				)
		end
	end

	connectStatsHumanoid()

	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		state.stats.lastPosition = nil
		task.wait(.2)
		connectStatsHumanoid()
	end)

	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		fpsFrames += 1

		local now = os.clock()
		local elapsed = now - fpsStarted

		if elapsed >= .5 then
			state.stats.fps =
				math.floor(fpsFrames / elapsed + .5)

			if state.stats.fps > 0 then
				state.stats.minFps =
					math.min(
						state.stats.minFps,
						state.stats.fps
					)
			end

			fpsFrames = 0
			fpsStarted = now
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			local currentRoot =
				player.Character
				and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if currentRoot then
				local position = currentRoot.Position
				local speed =
					Vector3.new(
						currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
						0,
						currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
					).Magnitude

				state.stats.maxSpeed =
					math.max(state.stats.maxSpeed,speed)

				if state.stats.lastPosition then
					local delta =
						(position-state.stats.lastPosition).Magnitude

					-- Ignore huge teleports when counting traveled distance.
					if delta < 120 then
						state.stats.distance += delta
					end
				end

				state.stats.lastPosition = position

				local seconds =
					math.floor(os.clock()-state.stats.started)
				local hours = math.floor(seconds/3600)
				local minutes =
					math.floor((seconds%3600)/60)
				local secs = seconds%60

				ui.statTime.Text =
					string.format(
						"%02d:%02d:%02d",
						hours,
						minutes,
						secs
					)
				ui.statDistance.Text =
					tostring(math.floor(state.stats.distance))
					.." studs"
				ui.statSpeed.Text =
					tostring(math.floor(speed+.5))
					.." / "
					..tostring(math.floor(state.stats.maxSpeed+.5))
				ui.statJumps.Text =
					tostring(state.stats.jumps)
				ui.statFps.Text =
					tostring(state.stats.fps)
					.." / "
					..(
						state.stats.minFps == 999
						and "--"
						or tostring(state.stats.minFps)
					)
				ui.statPlayers.Text =
					tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
			end

			task.wait(.2)
		end
	end)

	local translationBusy = {}
	local translatedObjects = {}

	local function getMenuLanguageCode()
		local code =
			string.lower(
				tostring(
					gui:GetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageCode") or "en"
				)
			)

		code = string.match(code,"^[a-z]+") or "en"

		if code ~= "ar" and code ~= "ru" then
			code = "en"
		end

		return code
	end

	local function exactTranslate(value,code)
		value = tostring(value or "")

		if code == "en" or value == "" then
			return value
		end

		local pack = exactTranslations[code]
		if not pack then
			return value
		end

		return pack[value] or value
	end

	local function translateDynamic(value,code)
		value = tostring(value or "")

		if code == "en" or value == "" then
			return value
		end

		local direct = exactTranslate(value,code)
		if direct ~= value then
			return direct
		end

		-- Dashboard character line:
		-- HP: 100 | State: WALKING | Position: 1, 2, 3
		local hpValue,stateValue,positionValue =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^HP:%s*([^|]+)%s*|%s*State:%s*([^|]+)%s*|%s*Position:%s*(.+)$"
			)

		if hpValue and stateValue and positionValue then
			hpValue =
				string.gsub(hpValue,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
			stateValue =
				string.gsub(stateValue,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
			positionValue =
				string.gsub(positionValue,"^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")

			return
				exactTranslate("HP",code)
				..": "..hpValue
				.."   |   "
				..exactTranslate("State",code)
				..": "..exactTranslate(stateValue,code)
				.."   |   "
				..exactTranslate("Position",code)
				..": "..positionValue
		end

		-- Live HUD with changing numbers.
		local hudFps,hudSpeed,hudX,hudY,hudZ,hudGravity =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^FPS:%s*([%-%d]+)%s+SPEED:%s*([%-%d]+)\nPOSITION:%s*X%s*([%-%d]+)%s+Y%s*([%-%d]+)%s+Z%s*([%-%d]+)\nGRAVITY:%s*([%-%d]+)$"
			)

		if hudFps
			and hudSpeed
			and hudX
			and hudY
			and hudZ
			and hudGravity then

			return
				"FPS: "..hudFps
				.."    "
				..exactTranslate("SPEED",code)
				..": "..hudSpeed
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("POSITION",code)
				..": X "..hudX
				.."   Y "..hudY
				.."   Z "..hudZ
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("GRAVITY",code)
				..": "..hudGravity
		end

		-- Profile details with dynamic account values.
		local profileUserId,profileAge =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^USER ID:%s*(%d+)\nACCOUNT AGE:%s*(%d+)%s+DAYS$"
			)

		if profileUserId and profileAge then
			return
				exactTranslate("USER ID",code)
				..": "..profileUserId
				.."\n"
				..exactTranslate("ACCOUNT AGE",code)
				..": "..profileAge
				.." "..exactTranslate("DAYS",code)
		end

		-- Tracker status with a changing marked-player count/name.
		local markedCount =
			string.match(
				value,
				"^Tracking all players%s+|%s+Marked:%s*(.+)$"
			)

		if markedCount then
			return
				exactTranslate("Tracking all players",code)
				.."  |  "
				..exactTranslate("Marked",code)
				..": "..markedCount
		end

		local trackedName =
			string.match(value,"^Tracking only @(.*)$")

		if trackedName then
			return
				exactTranslate("Tracking only",code)
				.." @"..trackedName
		end

		-- Detector signal lists are built dynamically from English phrases.
		local signalRest =
			string.match(value,"^SIGNALS:%s*(.+)$")

		if signalRest then
			local translatedSignalRest =
				exactTranslate(signalRest,code)

			if translatedSignalRest == signalRest
				and string.find(signalRest,", ",1,true) then

				local translatedSignals = {}
				local signalStart = 1

				while true do
					local first,last =
						string.find(
							signalRest,
							", ",
							signalStart,
							true
						)

					if not first then
						table.insert(
							translatedSignals,
							exactTranslate(
								string.sub(signalRest,signalStart),
								code
							)
						)
						break
					end

					table.insert(
						translatedSignals,
						exactTranslate(
							string.sub(signalRest,signalStart,first-1),
							code
						)
					)

					signalStart = last + 1
				end

				translatedSignalRest =
					table.concat(translatedSignals,", ")
			end

			return
				exactTranslate("SIGNALS",code)
				..": "..translatedSignalRest
		end

		-- Numbered controls/statuses such as WALK 16 or SAVE PRESET 1.
		local numberedPrefix,numberedValue =
			string.match(value,"^(.-)%s+([%-]?%d+%.?%d*)$")

		if numberedPrefix and numberedValue then
			local translatedPrefix =
				exactTranslate(numberedPrefix,code)

			if translatedPrefix ~= numberedPrefix then
				return translatedPrefix.." "..numberedValue
			end
		end

		local presetSlot =
			string.match(value,"^PRESET%s+(%d+)%s+SAVED$")

		if presetSlot then
			return
				exactTranslate("PRESET",code)
				.." "..presetSlot
				.." "..exactTranslate("SAVED",code)
		end

		-- FEATURE [ON/OFF/LOCKED]
		local feature,stateText =
			string.match(value,"^(.-) %[(.-)%]$")

		if feature and stateText then
			return
				exactTranslate(feature,code)
				.." ["
				..exactTranslate(stateText,code)
				.."]"
		end

		-- PREFIX: VALUE
		local prefix,rest =
			string.match(value,"^([^:]+):%s*(.*)$")

		if prefix and rest then
			local translatedPrefix = exactTranslate(prefix,code)
			local translatedRest = exactTranslate(rest,code)

			if translatedPrefix ~= prefix
				or translatedRest ~= rest then
				return translatedPrefix..": "..translatedRest
			end
		end

		-- STATUS // STATUS // dynamic-value
		if string.find(value," // ",1,true) then
			local pieces = {}
			local startAt = 1

			while true do
				local first,last =
					string.find(value," // ",startAt,true)

				if not first then
					table.insert(
						pieces,
						exactTranslate(
							string.sub(value,startAt),
							code
						)
					)
					break
				end

				table.insert(
					pieces,
					exactTranslate(
						string.sub(value,startAt,first-1),
						code
					)
				)

				startAt = last + 1
			end

			return table.concat(pieces," // ")
		end

		-- Safe dynamic prefixes. Only the fixed prefix is translated.
		local dynamicPrefixes = {
			"CURRENT: ",
			"PLAYERS: ",
			"DISTANCE: ",
			"USER ID: ",
			"EXECUTOR: ",
			"SIGNALS: ",
			"STATUS: ",
			"TARGET: ",
			"THEME: ",
			"OUTLINE: ",
			"TRACKING @",
			"INSPECTING @",
			"AIM TARGET // ",
			"WAYPOINT SAVED // ",
			"MONSTERS FOUND: ",
			"READY // "
		}

		for _,englishPrefix in ipairs(dynamicPrefixes) do
			if string.sub(value,1,#englishPrefix) == englishPrefix then
				local basePrefix =
					string.gsub(
						englishPrefix,
						"[:/@ ]+$",
						""
					)

				local translatedPrefix =
					exactTranslate(basePrefix,code)

				if translatedPrefix ~= basePrefix then
					local suffix =
						string.sub(value,#englishPrefix+1)

					local separator =
						string.sub(
							englishPrefix,
							#basePrefix+1
						)

					return
						translatedPrefix
						..separator
						..suffix
				end
			end
		end

		-- If a phrase was not explicitly translated, keep the original
		-- English intact instead of producing a broken mixed sentence.
		return value
	end

	local function applyTranslatedObject(object,force)
		if not object
			or not object.Parent
			or translationBusy[object] then
			return
		end

		local code = getMenuLanguageCode()

		if object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton") then

			local last =
				object:GetAttribute("NotRubyTranslatedText")
			local source =
				object:GetAttribute("NotRubyEnglishText")

			if not force and object.Text ~= last then
				source = object.Text
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishText",
					source
				)
			elseif source == nil then
				source = object.Text
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishText",
					source
				)
			end

			local translated =
				translateDynamic(source,code)

			translationBusy[object] = true
			object:SetAttribute(
				"NotRubyTranslatedText",
				translated
			)
			object.Text = translated
			translationBusy[object] = nil

		elseif object:IsA("TextBox") then
			local last =
				object:GetAttribute(
					"NotRubyTranslatedPlaceholder"
				)
			local source =
				object:GetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishPlaceholder"
				)

			if not force
				and object.PlaceholderText ~= last then
				source = object.PlaceholderText
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishPlaceholder",
					source
				)
			elseif source == nil then
				source = object.PlaceholderText
				object:SetAttribute(
					"NotRubyEnglishPlaceholder",
					source
				)
			end

			local translated =
				translateDynamic(source,code)

			translationBusy[object] = true
			object:SetAttribute(
				"NotRubyTranslatedPlaceholder",
				translated
			)
			object.PlaceholderText = translated
			translationBusy[object] = nil
		end
	end

	local function watchTranslatedObject(object)
		if translatedObjects[object] then
			return
		end

		if not (
			object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton")
			or object:IsA("TextBox")
		) then
			return
		end

		translatedObjects[object] = true
		applyTranslatedObject(object,false)

		if object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton") then
			object:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(
				function()
					applyTranslatedObject(object,false)
				end
			)
		else
			object:GetPropertyChangedSignal(
				"PlaceholderText"
			):Connect(function()
				applyTranslatedObject(object,false)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function applyAllMenuTranslations()
		for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			watchTranslatedObject(object)
			applyTranslatedObject(object,true)
		end
	end

	gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
		task.defer(function()
			if object and object.Parent then
				watchTranslatedObject(object)
				applyTranslatedObject(object,true)
			end
		end)
	end)

	gui:GetAttributeChangedSignal(
		"WelcomeLanguageCode"
	):Connect(function()
		task.defer(applyAllMenuTranslations)
	end)

	applyAllMenuTranslations()

	local function applyLanguageChoice()
		local query = string.lower(
			string.gsub(languageInput.Text or "","^%s*(.-)%s*$","%1")
		)

		if query == "" then
			setStatus("TYPE A LANGUAGE OR COUNTRY")
			return
		end

		local choice = nil

		for _,entry in ipairs(languageChoices) do
			local haystack =
				string.lower(
					entry.code
					.." "
					..entry.name
					.." "
					..entry.country
					.." "
					..tostring(entry.aliases or "")
				)

			if query == string.lower(entry.code)
				or query == string.lower(entry.name)
				or query == string.lower(entry.country)
				or string.find(haystack,query,1,true) then
				choice = entry
				break
			end
		end

		if not choice then
			setStatus("LANGUAGE NOT FOUND")
			return
		end

		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageCode",choice.code)
		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageName",choice.name)
		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeCountry",choice.country)

		if type(writefile) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				writefile(languageFile,choice.code)
			end)
		end

		languageInput.Text = ""
		languageCurrent.Text = "CURRENT: "..choice.name
		applyAllMenuTranslations()
		setStatus("LANGUAGE CHANGED // "..string.upper(choice.name))
	end

	languageApply.Activated:Connect(applyLanguageChoice)

	languageInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		if enterPressed then
			applyLanguageChoice()
		end
	end)

	-- Custom color picker // touch-friendly circular palette

	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
		"CUSTOM COLOR PICKER",
		"Tap a color in the circle, then apply it to the menu theme or outline."
