	local colorPickerFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	colorPickerFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,260)
	colorPickerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
	colorPickerFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	colorPickerFrame.Parent = settingsPage
	corner(colorPickerFrame,11)

	-- The palette is built from concentric HSV rings so it works without
	-- depending on an external color-wheel image asset.
	local colorWheel = Instance.new("Frame")
	colorWheel.Name = "NotRubyColorWheel"
	colorWheel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,0)
	colorWheel.Position = UDim2.new(.5,0,0,10)
	colorWheel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(172,172)
	colorWheel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	colorWheel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	colorWheel.Parent = colorPickerFrame

	local colorWheelAspect = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
	colorWheelAspect.AspectRatio = 1
	colorWheelAspect.Parent = colorWheel

	local selectedColor = accent
	local selectedSwatch = nil

	local preview = Instance.new("Frame")
	preview.Name = "SelectedColorPreview"
	preview.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
	preview.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
	preview.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42,42)
	preview.BackgroundColor3 = selectedColor
	preview.BorderSizePixel = 0
	preview.ZIndex = 8
	preview.Parent = colorWheel
	corner(preview,1000)
	local previewStroke =
		addStroke(
			preview,
			gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
			2,
			.05
		)

	local function choosePickerColor(color,swatch)
		selectedColor = color
		preview.BackgroundColor3 = color

		if selectedSwatch
			and selectedSwatch.Parent then
			local oldStroke =
				selectedSwatch:FindFirstChild("PickerSelection")
			if oldStroke then
				oldStroke:Destroy()
			end
		end

		selectedSwatch = swatch
		if swatch and swatch.Parent then
			local selection = Instance.new("UIStroke")
			selection.Name = "PickerSelection"
			selection.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
			selection.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
			selection.Thickness = 2.4
			selection.Transparency = 0
			selection.Parent = swatch
		end
	end

	-- Four saturation rings. Outer = strongest color, inner = softer.
	for ring = 1,4 do
		local saturation = ring/4
		local radius = 23 + ring*14
		local swatchSize = 17

		for segment = 0,23 do
			local hue = segment/24
			local angle = hue*math.pi*2-math.pi/2
			local x = math.cos(angle)*radius
			local y = math.sin(angle)*radius
			local color = Color3.fromHSV(hue,saturation,1)

			local swatch = Instance.new("TextButton")
			swatch.Name = "ColorSwatch"
			swatch.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
			swatch.Position =
				UDim2.new(.5,x,.5,y)
			swatch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(swatchSize,swatchSize)
			swatch.Text = ""
			swatch.BackgroundColor3 = color
			swatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
			swatch.AutoButtonColor = false
			swatch.ZIndex = 5
			swatch.Parent = colorWheel
			corner(swatch,1000)

			swatch.Activated:Connect(function()
				choosePickerColor(color,swatch)
			end)
		end
	end

	-- Neutral colors are useful for outlines/themes and keep the picker simple.
	local neutralRow = Instance.new("Frame")
	neutralRow.Position = UDim2.new(.5,-86,0,186)
	neutralRow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(172,24)
	neutralRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	neutralRow.Parent = colorPickerFrame

	for index,color in ipairs({
		Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
		Color3.fromRGB(190,190,190),
		Color3.fromRGB(120,120,120),
		Color3.fromRGB(55,55,55),
		Color3.fromRGB(8,8,8)
	}) do
		local swatch = Instance.new("TextButton")
		swatch.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
		swatch.Position =
			UDim2.new(
				(index-.5)/5,
				0,
				.5,
				0
			)
		swatch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22,22)
		swatch.Text = ""
		swatch.BackgroundColor3 = color
		swatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
		swatch.AutoButtonColor = false
		swatch.Parent = neutralRow
		corner(swatch,1000)
		addStroke(
			swatch,
			gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
			1,
			.15
		)

		swatch.Activated:Connect(function()
			choosePickerColor(color,swatch)
		end)
	end

	ui.applyRgbTheme = Instance.new("TextButton")
	ui.applyRgbTheme.Position = UDim2.new(0,7,1,-43)
	ui.applyRgbTheme.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-10,0,35)
	ui.applyRgbTheme.Text = "APPLY COLOR THEME"
	ui.applyRgbTheme.TextSize = 8
	ui.applyRgbTheme.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	ui.applyRgbTheme.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.applyRgbTheme.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.applyRgbTheme.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.applyRgbTheme.AutoButtonColor = false
	ui.applyRgbTheme.Parent = colorPickerFrame
	corner(ui.applyRgbTheme,8)

	ui.applyRgbOutline = Instance.new("TextButton")
	ui.applyRgbOutline.Position = UDim2.new(.5,3,1,-43)
	ui.applyRgbOutline.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-10,0,35)
	ui.applyRgbOutline.Text = "APPLY COLOR OUTLINE"
	ui.applyRgbOutline.TextSize = 8
	ui.applyRgbOutline.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	ui.applyRgbOutline.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.applyRgbOutline.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.applyRgbOutline.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.applyRgbOutline.AutoButtonColor = false
	ui.applyRgbOutline.Parent = colorPickerFrame
	corner(ui.applyRgbOutline,8)

	local function applyCustomTheme(color)
		accent = color
		themeIndex = 0
		themeButton.Text = "THEME: CUSTOM"
		status.TextColor3 = accent
		sidebar.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent

		applyTabVisuals()
		refreshToggleColors()

		if gui:GetAttribute("OutlineMode") == "MATCH" then
			gui:SetAttribute("OutlineColor",accent)
			for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
				if object:IsA("UIStroke") then
					object.Color = accent
				end
			end
		end

		previewStroke.Color =
			gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent

		saveStorage()
		setStatus("CUSTOM COLOR THEME APPLIED")
	end

	ui.applyRgbTheme.Activated:Connect(function()
		applyCustomTheme(selectedColor)
	end)

	ui.applyRgbOutline.Activated:Connect(function()
		gui:SetAttribute("OutlineMode","CUSTOM")
		gui:SetAttribute("OutlineIndex",0)
		gui:SetAttribute("OutlineColor",selectedColor)
		outlineButton.Text = "OUTLINE: CUSTOM"

		for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("UIStroke")
				and object.Name ~= "PickerSelection" then
				object.Color = selectedColor
			end
		end

		previewStroke.Color = selectedColor
		saveStorage()
		setStatus("CUSTOM COLOR OUTLINE APPLIED")
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Keybind editor
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
		"KEYBINDS",
		"Keyboard shortcuts for Flight, Noclip, Invisible, Fake Lag and Wall Parts. Tap a box and enter a key name."
	)

	ui.keybindBoxes = {}

	local function makeKeybindRow(feature)
		local row = Instance.new("Frame")
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42)
		row.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE
		row.BorderSizePixel = 0
		row.Parent = settingsPage
		corner(row,9)

		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10,0)
		label.Size = UDim2.new(.58,-10,1,0)
		label.Text = feature
		label.TextSize = 9
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		label.TextColor3 = TEXT
		label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		label.Parent = row

		local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
		box.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,.5)
		box.Position = UDim2.new(1,-8,.5,0)
		box.Size = UDim2.new(.35,0,0,30)
		box.Text =
			string.upper(
				tostring(state.data.keybinds[feature] or "")
			)
		box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
		box.TextSize = 9
		box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		box.TextColor3 = accent
		box.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		box.BorderSizePixel = 0
		box.Parent = row
		corner(box,7)

		ui.keybindBoxes[feature] = box

		box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
			local value =
				string.upper(
					string.gsub(
						box.Text or "",
						"%s+",
						""
					)
				)

			local keyCode = Enum.KeyCode[value]

			if not keyCode
				or keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
				box.Text =
					tostring(
						state.data.keybinds[feature]
						or ""
					)
				notify("INVALID KEY // "..feature)
				return
			end

			state.data.keybinds[feature] = value
			box.Text = value
			saveStorage()
			notify(feature.." KEYBIND = "..value)
		end)
	end

	for _,feature in ipairs({
		"Flight",
		"Noclip",
		"Invisible",
		"FakeLag",
		"WallParts"
	}) do
		makeKeybindRow(feature)
	end

	local function triggerCommand(feature)
		if feature == "Flight" then
			setFlight(not flying)
		elseif feature == "Noclip" then
			setNoclip(not noclip)
		elseif feature == "Invisible" then
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"CommandInvisible",
				(tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("CommandInvisible")) or 0)+1
			)
		elseif feature == "FakeLag" then
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"CommandFakeLag",
				(tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("CommandFakeLag")) or 0)+1
			)
		elseif feature == "WallParts" then
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"WallPartsVisual",
				not (gui:GetAttribute("WallPartsVisual") == true)
			)
		end
	end

	UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input,processed)
		if processed
			or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox()
			or input.UserInputType
				~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			return
		end

		local pressed = input.KeyCode.Name

		for feature,keyName in pairs(state.data.keybinds) do
			if pressed == keyName then
				triggerCommand(feature)
				return
			end
		end
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Presets: 3 save/load slots
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
		"PRESETS",
		"Save up to 3 feature setups. Presets remember movement values, main toggles, FOV, locators, wall visuals, Fake Lag and Invisible state."
	)

	local function capturePreset()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

		return {
			tab = currentTab,
			walk = walkSpeed,
			jump = jumpPower,
			sprintSpeed = sprintSpeed,
			flySpeed = flySpeed,
			radius = scanRadius,
			fov = camera and camera.FieldOfView or originalFOV,
			flight = flying,
			sprint = sprinting,
			noclip = noclip,
			night = nightVision,
			bright = fullBright,
			xray = xrayMode,
			players = playerLocator,
			monsters = monsterLocator,
			wall = gui:GetAttribute("WallPartsVisual") == true,
			fakeLag = gui:GetAttribute("FakeLagEnabled") == true,
			invisible = gui:GetAttribute("InvisibleEnabled") == true
		}
	end

	local function applyPreset(preset)
		if typeof(preset) ~= "table" then
			notify("PRESET SLOT IS EMPTY")
			return
		end

		walkSpeed =
			math.clamp(tonumber(preset.walk) or 16,1,250)
		jumpPower =
			math.clamp(tonumber(preset.jump) or 50,0,250)
		sprintSpeed =
			math.clamp(
				tonumber(preset.sprintSpeed) or 42,
				1,
				300
			)
		flySpeed =
			math.clamp(
				tonumber(preset.flySpeed) or 70,
				1,
				400
			)
		scanRadius =
			math.clamp(
				tonumber(preset.radius) or 5000,
				100,
				50000
			)

		walkBox.Text = tostring(walkSpeed)
		jumpBox.Text = tostring(jumpPower)
		sprintBox.Text = tostring(sprintSpeed)
		flyBox.Text = tostring(flySpeed)
		radiusBox.Text = tostring(scanRadius)

		applyMovementStats()
		setFlight(preset.flight == true)
		setSprint(preset.sprint == true)
		setNoclip(preset.noclip == true)
		setNightVision(preset.night == true)
		setFullBright(preset.bright == true)
		setXray(preset.xray == true)
		setPlayerLocator(preset.players == true)
		setMonsterLocator(preset.monsters == true)

		gui:SetAttribute(
			"WallPartsVisual",
			preset.wall == true
		)

		if (gui:GetAttribute("FakeLagEnabled") == true)
			~= (preset.fakeLag == true) then
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"CommandFakeLag",
				(tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("CommandFakeLag")) or 0)+1
			)
		end

		if (gui:GetAttribute("InvisibleEnabled") == true)
			~= (preset.invisible == true) then
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"CommandInvisible",
				(tonumber(gui:GetAttribute("CommandInvisible")) or 0)+1
			)
		end

		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera then
			camera.FieldOfView =
				math.clamp(
					tonumber(preset.fov) or originalFOV,
					40,
					120
				)
			fovBox.Text =
				tostring(
					math.floor(camera.FieldOfView)
				)
		end

		if type(preset.tab) == "string"
			and pages[preset.tab] then
			setTab(preset.tab)
		end

		notify("PRESET LOADED")
	end

	for slot = 1,3 do
		local row = Instance.new("Frame")
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42)
		row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		row.Parent = settingsPage

		local saveButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
		saveButton.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
		saveButton.Text = "SAVE PRESET "..slot
		saveButton.TextSize = 8
		saveButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		saveButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
		saveButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		saveButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
		saveButton.AutoButtonColor = false
		saveButton.Parent = row
		corner(saveButton,9)

		local loadButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
		loadButton.Position = UDim2.new(.5,4,0,0)
		loadButton.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
		loadButton.Text = "LOAD PRESET "..slot
		loadButton.TextSize = 8
		loadButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		loadButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
		loadButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		loadButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
		loadButton.AutoButtonColor = false
		loadButton.Parent = row
		corner(loadButton,9)

		saveButton.Activated:Connect(function()
			state.data.presets[tostring(slot)] =
				capturePreset()
			saveStorage()
			notify("PRESET "..slot.." SAVED")
		end)

		loadButton.Activated:Connect(function()
			applyPreset(
				state.data.presets[tostring(slot)]
			)
		end)
	end

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Error sound settings
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
		"ERROR SYSTEM",
		"Error notifications use a warning sound. Turn it off anytime, or change the sound and hear a preview immediately."
	)

	local errorSoundToggle,errorSoundChange =
		makePair(
			settingsPage,
			"ERROR SOUND [ON]",
			"CHANGE SOUND: "..currentErrorSoundName()
		)

	local customSoundIdBox,customSoundApply =
		makeNumberRow(
			settingsPage,
			"CUSTOM SOUND ID",
			customErrorSoundId,
			"USE + PREVIEW"
		)

	customSoundIdBox.PlaceholderText = "PASTE ROBLOX AUDIO ID"

	setToggle(
		errorSoundToggle,
		errorSoundEnabled,
		"ERROR SOUND"
	)

	errorSoundToggle.Activated:Connect(function()
		errorSoundEnabled = not errorSoundEnabled
		gui:SetAttribute("ErrorSoundEnabled",errorSoundEnabled)
		state.data.settings.errorSoundEnabled = errorSoundEnabled

		setToggle(
			errorSoundToggle,
			errorSoundEnabled,
			"ERROR SOUND"
		)

		saveStorage()
		notify(
			errorSoundEnabled
			and "ERROR SOUND ON"
			or "ERROR SOUND OFF"
		)
	end)

	errorSoundChange.Activated:Connect(function()
		useCustomErrorSound = false
		state.data.settings.useCustomErrorSound = false

		errorSoundIndex += 1
		if errorSoundIndex > #errorSoundOptions then
			errorSoundIndex = 1
		end

		local selectedSound = errorSoundOptions[errorSoundIndex]
		errorSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..selectedSound.id
		gui:SetAttribute("ErrorSoundId",selectedSound.id)
		state.data.settings.errorSoundIndex = errorSoundIndex

		errorSoundChange.Text =
			"CHANGE SOUND: "..selectedSound.name

		saveStorage()
		playErrorSound(true)
		notify("SOUND PREVIEW // "..selectedSound.name)
	end)

	customSoundApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local enteredId =
			tostring(customSoundIdBox.Text or "")
				:gsub("%D","")

		-- Roblox audio asset IDs are numeric. Keep a broad digit length
		-- range so older and newer asset IDs both work.
		if enteredId == ""
			or #enteredId < 5
			or #enteredId > 20 then
			notify(
				"CUSTOM SOUND ERROR // ENTER A VALID ROBLOX AUDIO ID"
			)
			return
		end

		customErrorSoundId = enteredId
		useCustomErrorSound = true
		customSoundIdBox.Text = enteredId

		state.data.settings.customErrorSoundId =
			customErrorSoundId
		state.data.settings.useCustomErrorSound = true

		errorSound.SoundId =
			"rbxassetid://"..customErrorSoundId
		gui:SetAttribute(
			"ErrorSoundId",
			customErrorSoundId
		)

		errorSoundChange.Text =
			"CHANGE SOUND: CUSTOM "..customErrorSoundId

		saveStorage()
		playErrorSound(true)
		notify(
			"CUSTOM SOUND PREVIEW // "..customErrorSoundId
		)
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Settings persistence
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	makeSection(
		settingsPage,
		"PERSISTENCE",
		state.canFiles
			and "Auto-saves theme, outline, panel/HUD/mini layout, favorites, keybinds, waypoints and presets on this account/device."
			or "Permanent file storage is unavailable here. Settings will last only for this session."
	)

	local persistenceActions = Instance.new("Frame")
	persistenceActions.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,42)
	persistenceActions.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	persistenceActions.Parent = settingsPage

	ui.saveSettingsNow = Instance.new("TextButton")
	ui.saveSettingsNow.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
	ui.saveSettingsNow.Text = "SAVE SETTINGS NOW"
	ui.saveSettingsNow.TextSize = 8
	ui.saveSettingsNow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	ui.saveSettingsNow.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.saveSettingsNow.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.saveSettingsNow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.saveSettingsNow.AutoButtonColor = false
	ui.saveSettingsNow.Parent = persistenceActions
	corner(ui.saveSettingsNow,9)

	ui.clearSavedSettings = Instance.new("TextButton")
	ui.clearSavedSettings.Position = UDim2.new(.5,4,0,0)
	ui.clearSavedSettings.Size = UDim2.new(.5,-4,1,0)
	ui.clearSavedSettings.Text = "CLEAR SAVED SETTINGS"
	ui.clearSavedSettings.TextSize = 8
	ui.clearSavedSettings.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	ui.clearSavedSettings.TextColor3 = TEXT
	ui.clearSavedSettings.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	ui.clearSavedSettings.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ui.clearSavedSettings.AutoButtonColor = false
	ui.clearSavedSettings.Parent = persistenceActions
	corner(ui.clearSavedSettings,9)

	local function colorToData(color)
		return {
			r = math.floor(color.R*255+.5),
			g = math.floor(color.G*255+.5),
			b = math.floor(color.B*255+.5)
		}
	end

	local function dataToColor(data,fallback)
		if typeof(data) ~= "table" then
			return fallback
		end

		return Color3.fromRGB(
			math.clamp(tonumber(data.r) or 0,0,255),
			math.clamp(tonumber(data.g) or 0,0,255),
			math.clamp(tonumber(data.b) or 0,0,255)
		)
	end

	local function captureSettings()
		local saved = {
			themeIndex = themeIndex,
			accent = colorToData(accent),
			errorSoundEnabled = errorSoundEnabled,
			errorSoundIndex = errorSoundIndex,
			customErrorSoundId = customErrorSoundId,
			useCustomErrorSound = useCustomErrorSound,
			outlineMode =
				gui:GetAttribute("OutlineMode"),
			outlineIndex =
				gui:GetAttribute("OutlineIndex"),
			outline =
				colorToData(
					gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor")
					or accent
				),
			panel = {
				xs = panel.Position.X.Scale,
				xo = panel.Position.X.Offset,
				ys = panel.Position.Y.Scale,
				yo = panel.Position.Y.Offset,
				w = panel.AbsoluteSize.X,
				h = panel.AbsoluteSize.Y
			},
			mini = {
				xs = openButton.Position.X.Scale,
				xo = openButton.Position.X.Offset,
				ys = openButton.Position.Y.Scale,
				yo = openButton.Position.Y.Offset,
				w = openButton.AbsoluteSize.X,
				h = openButton.AbsoluteSize.Y
			}
		}

		local hud = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyLiveHUD")
		if hud and hud:IsA("GuiObject") then
			saved.hud = {
				xs = hud.Position.X.Scale,
				xo = hud.Position.X.Offset,
				ys = hud.Position.Y.Scale,
				yo = hud.Position.Y.Offset,
				w = hud.AbsoluteSize.X,
				h = hud.AbsoluteSize.Y
			}
		end

		state.data.settings = saved
	end

	local function applySavedSettings()
		local saved = state.data.settings
		if typeof(saved) ~= "table" then
			return
		end

		if saved.errorSoundEnabled ~= nil then
			errorSoundEnabled = saved.errorSoundEnabled == true
		end

		if tonumber(saved.errorSoundIndex) then
			errorSoundIndex =
				math.clamp(
					math.floor(tonumber(saved.errorSoundIndex)),
					1,
					#errorSoundOptions
				)
		end

		if saved.customErrorSoundId ~= nil then
			customErrorSoundId =
				tostring(saved.customErrorSoundId or "")
					:gsub("%D","")
		end

		useCustomErrorSound =
			saved.useCustomErrorSound == true
			and customErrorSoundId ~= ""

		customSoundIdBox.Text = customErrorSoundId

		errorSound.SoundId =
			"rbxassetid://"..currentErrorSoundId()
		gui:SetAttribute("ErrorSoundEnabled",errorSoundEnabled)
		gui:SetAttribute(
			"ErrorSoundId",
			currentErrorSoundId()
		)
		setToggle(
			errorSoundToggle,
			errorSoundEnabled,
			"ERROR SOUND"
		)
		errorSoundChange.Text =
			"CHANGE SOUND: "..currentErrorSoundName()

		if saved.accent then
			accent =
				dataToColor(saved.accent,accent)

			local savedIndex =
				tonumber(saved.themeIndex)

			if savedIndex
				and savedIndex >= 1
				and savedIndex <= #themes then
				themeIndex = savedIndex
				themeButton.Text =
					"THEME: "
					..themes[themeIndex].name
			else
				themeIndex = 0
				themeButton.Text = "THEME: CUSTOM"
			end

			status.TextColor3 = accent
			sidebar.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
			applyTabVisuals()
			refreshToggleColors()
		end

		if saved.outline then
			local outline =
				dataToColor(saved.outline,accent)

			gui:SetAttribute(
				"OutlineMode",
				saved.outlineMode or "MATCH"
			)
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"OutlineIndex",
				tonumber(saved.outlineIndex) or 1
			)
			gui:SetAttribute("OutlineColor",outline)

			for _,object in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
				if object:IsA("UIStroke") then
					object.Color = outline
				end
			end

			outlineButton.Text =
				saved.outlineMode == "CUSTOM"
				and "OUTLINE: CUSTOM"
				or "OUTLINE: MATCH"
		end

		if typeof(saved.panel) == "table" then
			panel.Position =
				UDim2.new(
					tonumber(saved.panel.xs) or .5,
					tonumber(saved.panel.xo) or -280,
					tonumber(saved.panel.ys) or .5,
					tonumber(saved.panel.yo) or -215
				)
			panel.Size =
				UDim2.fromOffset(
					math.max(
						320,
						tonumber(saved.panel.w) or 560
					),
					math.max(
						250,
						tonumber(saved.panel.h) or 430
					)
				)
		end

		if typeof(saved.mini) == "table" then
			openButton.Position =
				UDim2.new(
					tonumber(saved.mini.xs) or 0,
					tonumber(saved.mini.xo) or 9,
					tonumber(saved.mini.ys) or .5,
					tonumber(saved.mini.yo) or -26
				)

			local size =
				math.clamp(
					tonumber(saved.mini.w) or 52,
					34,
					140
				)

			openButton.Size =
				UDim2.fromOffset(size,size)
			local restoredMiniCorner =
				openButton:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
			if restoredMiniCorner then
				restoredMiniCorner.CornerRadius =
					UDim.new(.5,0)
			end
			openButton.TextSize =
				math.clamp(
					math.floor(size*.46),
					16,
					42
				)
		end

		local hud = gui:FindFirstChild("NotRubyLiveHUD")
		if hud
			and hud:IsA("GuiObject")
			and typeof(saved.hud) == "table" then

			hud.Position =
				UDim2.new(
					tonumber(saved.hud.xs) or 0,
					tonumber(saved.hud.xo) or 18,
					tonumber(saved.hud.ys) or 0,
					tonumber(saved.hud.yo) or 82
				)
			hud.Size =
				UDim2.fromOffset(
					math.max(
						210,
						tonumber(saved.hud.w) or 300
					),
					math.max(
						100,
						tonumber(saved.hud.h) or 128
					)
				)
		end
	end

	ui.saveSettingsNow.Activated:Connect(function()
		captureSettings()
		if saveStorage() then
			notify("SETTINGS SAVED")
		else
			notify("FILE STORAGE UNAVAILABLE")
		end
	end)

	ui.clearSavedSettings.Activated:Connect(function()
		if state.canFiles
			and type(delfile) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				if isfile(state.file) then
					delfile(state.file)
				end
			end)
		end

		state.data.settings = {}
		errorSoundEnabled = true
		errorSoundIndex = 1
		customErrorSoundId = ""
		useCustomErrorSound = false
		customSoundIdBox.Text = ""
		errorSound.SoundId =
			"rbxassetid://"..currentErrorSoundId()
		gui:SetAttribute("ErrorSoundEnabled",true)
		gui:SetAttribute(
			"ErrorSoundId",
			currentErrorSoundId()
		)
		setToggle(errorSoundToggle,true,"ERROR SOUND")
		errorSoundChange.Text =
			"CHANGE SOUND: "..currentErrorSoundName()
		state.data.favorites = {}
		state.data.presets = {}
		state.data.waypoints = {}
		rebuildWaypoints()
		rebuildFavorites()
		notify("SAVED NOT RUBY SETTINGS CLEARED")
	end)

	applySavedSettings()

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Scan feature buttons only after all extra Settings rows exist.
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	task.defer(function()
		task.wait(.15)
		scanFeatureButtons()
		rebuildFavorites()
	end)

	-- Automatically instrument feature buttons created later.
	for pageName,page in pairs(pages) do
		page.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
			if object:IsA("TextButton")
				and object.Name ~= "FavoriteStar"
				and not object.Parent:IsA("TextButton") then
				task.defer(function()
					if object.Parent then
						makeFavoriteStar(object,pageName)
						rebuildFavorites()
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Pages such as SHOOTER are created after Power Suite loads.
	-- Watch the Content container too so late-added tabs still join Search
	-- and Favorites instead of being invisible to those systems.
	content.ChildAdded:Connect(function(newPage)
		if not newPage:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
			return
		end

		task.defer(function()
			task.wait()
			local pageName =
				string.gsub(newPage.Name,"Page$","")

			for _,object in ipairs(newPage:GetDescendants()) do
				if object:IsA("TextButton")
					and object.Name ~= "FavoriteStar"
					and not object.Parent:IsA("TextButton") then
					makeFavoriteStar(object,pageName)
				end
			end

			newPage.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
				if object:IsA("TextButton")
					and object.Name ~= "FavoriteStar"
					and not object.Parent:IsA("TextButton") then
					task.defer(function()
						if object.Parent then
							makeFavoriteStar(object,pageName)
							rebuildFavorites()
						end
					end)
				end
			end)

			rebuildFavorites()
		end)
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- Autosave
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			task.wait(8)
			captureSettings()
			saveStorage()
		end
	end)

	gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
		captureSettings()
		saveStorage()
		destroyWaypointObjects()
		if errorSound and errorSound.Parent then
			errorSound:Destroy()
		end
	end)

	notify("POWER TOOLS LOADED // SEARCH + FAVORITES + PRESETS + MORE")
end
setupPowerSuite()


local function setupProfileIntro()
	local entering = false
	panel.Visible = false

	local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")

	-- Per-account visit counter. On environments with file persistence,
	-- visit 1 says normal welcome; visit 2+ says WELCOME BACK // VISIT #N.
	local visitCount = 1
	local visitFile =
		"not_ruby_v4_visits_"..tostring(player.UserId)..".txt"
	local languageFile =
		"not_ruby_v4_language_"..tostring(player.UserId)..".txt"

	do
		if type(isfile) == "function"
			and type(readfile) == "function"
			and type(writefile) == "function" then

			pcall(function()
				local previous = 0

				if isfile(visitFile) then
					previous =
						tonumber(readfile(visitFile))
						or 0
				end

				visitCount = math.max(1,previous + 1)
				writefile(visitFile,tostring(visitCount))
			end)
		end
	end

	gui:SetAttribute("NotRubyVisitCount",visitCount)

	-- Flags are generated with Regional Indicator Unicode characters.
	-- This avoids hard-coding flag emoji bytes into the source.
	local function countryFlag(countryCode)
		countryCode = string.upper(tostring(countryCode or ""))
		if #countryCode ~= 2 then
			return ""
		end

		local first = string.byte(countryCode,1)
		local second = string.byte(countryCode,2)

		if not first or not second
			or first < 65 or first > 90
			or second < 65 or second > 90 then
			return ""
		end

		return utf8.char(
			0x1F1E6 + first - 65,
			0x1F1E6 + second - 65
		)
	end

	-- 3 supported welcome languages: English, Arabic and Russian.
	-- "aliases" lets the country search recognize common countries that
	-- normally use the same supported language.
	local languages = {
		{
			code = "en",
			name = "English",
			country = "United States",
			countryCode = "US",
			aliases = "United States USA US America United Kingdom UK Britain England Canada Australia New Zealand Ireland Singapore Philippines",
			language = "LANGUAGE",
			welcome = "WELCOME TO NOT RUBY V1",
			subtitle = "Choose a language, or search for your country.",
			search = "Search your country...",
			enter = "ENTER NOT RUBY"
		},
		{
			code = "ar",
			name = "العربية",
			country = "Saudi Arabia",
			countryCode = "SA",
			aliases = "Arabic عربي العربية Saudi Arabia السعودية KSA UAE United Arab Emirates الإمارات Egypt مصر Jordan الأردن Kuwait الكويت Qatar قطر Bahrain البحرين Oman عمان Iraq العراق Lebanon لبنان Syria سوريا Yemen اليمن Palestine فلسطين Morocco المغرب Algeria الجزائر Tunisia تونس Libya ليبيا Sudan السودان",
			language = "اللغة",
			welcome = "مرحباً بك في NOT RUBY V1",
			subtitle = "اختر لغة أو ابحث عن بلدك.",
			search = "ابحث عن بلدك...",
			enter = "دخول NOT RUBY"
		},
		{
			code = "ru",
			name = "Русский",
			country = "Russia",
			countryCode = "RU",
			aliases = "Russian Русский Russia Россия Russian Federation Беларусь Belarus Kazakhstan Казахстан Kyrgyzstan Кыргызстан",
			language = "ЯЗЫК",
			welcome = "ДОБРО ПОЖАЛОВАТЬ В NOT RUBY V1",
			subtitle = "Выберите язык или найдите свою страну.",
			search = "Найти страну...",
			enter = "ВОЙТИ В NOT RUBY"
		}
	}

	local intro = Instance.new("Frame")
	intro.Name = "NotRubyWelcome"
	intro.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	intro.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(3,4,7)
	intro.BackgroundTransparency = .12
	intro.BorderSizePixel = 0
	intro.ZIndex = 500

	introCard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
	introCard.BackgroundColor3 = BG
	introCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
	introCard.ClipsDescendants = false
	introCard.ZIndex = 501
	introCard.Parent = intro
	corner(introCard,20)
	addStroke(
		introCard,
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.8,
		.02
	)

	local introTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	introTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	introTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,12)
	introTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,23)
	introTitle.Text = "NOT RUBY V1"
	introTitle.TextSize = 12
	introTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	introTitle.TextColor3 = TEXT
	introTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	introTitle.ZIndex = 502
	introTitle.Parent = introCard

	local welcomeText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	welcomeText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	welcomeText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,37)
	welcomeText.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,27)
	welcomeText.Text = "WELCOME TO NOT RUBY V1"
	welcomeText.TextSize = 13
	welcomeText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	welcomeText.TextColor3 = accent
	welcomeText.TextWrapped = true
	welcomeText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	welcomeText.ZIndex = 502
	welcomeText.Parent = introCard

	local welcomeSubtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	welcomeSubtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	welcomeSubtitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(22,64)
	welcomeSubtitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-44,0,27)
	welcomeSubtitle.Text = "Choose a language, or search for your country."
	welcomeSubtitle.TextSize = 8
	welcomeSubtitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	welcomeSubtitle.TextColor3 = MUTED
	welcomeSubtitle.TextWrapped = true
	welcomeSubtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	welcomeSubtitle.ZIndex = 502

	local introAvatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	introAvatar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,0)
	introAvatar.Position = UDim2.new(.5,0,0,94)

	introAvatar.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2

	addStroke(

		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.5,
		.05
	)

	local introName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	introName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	introName.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,170)
	introName.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,22)
	introName.Text = player.DisplayName
	introName.TextSize = 14
	introName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	introName.TextColor3 = TEXT
	introName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	introName.ZIndex = 502
	introName.Parent = introCard

	local introUser = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	introUser.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	introUser.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,191)
	introUser.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,16)
	introUser.Text = "@"..player.Name
	introUser.TextSize = 8
	introUser.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	introUser.TextColor3 = MUTED
	introUser.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	introUser.ZIndex = 502
	introUser.Parent = introCard

	local languageCaption = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	languageCaption.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	languageCaption.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,210)
	languageCaption.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,17)
	languageCaption.Text = "LANGUAGE"
	languageCaption.TextSize = 8
	languageCaption.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	languageCaption.TextColor3 = MUTED
	languageCaption.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	languageCaption.ZIndex = 502
	languageCaption.Parent = introCard

	local languageRow = Instance.new("Frame")
	languageRow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,229)
	languageRow.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,42)
	languageRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	languageRow.ZIndex = 502
	languageRow.Parent = introCard

	local previousLanguage = Instance.new("TextButton")
	previousLanguage.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42,42)
	previousLanguage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
	previousLanguage.Text = "<"
	previousLanguage.TextSize = 16
	previousLanguage.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	previousLanguage.TextColor3 = TEXT
	previousLanguage.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	previousLanguage.BorderSizePixel = 0
	previousLanguage.AutoButtonColor = false
	previousLanguage.ZIndex = 503
	previousLanguage.Parent = languageRow
	corner(previousLanguage,10)

	local languageButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

	languageButton.Size = UDim2.new(1,-98,0,42)
	languageButton.Text = ""
	languageButton.TextSize = 9
	languageButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	languageButton.TextColor3 = TEXT
	languageButton.TextWrapped = true

	languageButton.BorderSizePixel = 0

		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.2,
		.08
	)

	local nextLanguage = Instance.new("TextButton")
	nextLanguage.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
	nextLanguage.Position = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
	nextLanguage.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42,42)
	nextLanguage.Text = ">"
	nextLanguage.TextSize = 16
	nextLanguage.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	nextLanguage.TextColor3 = TEXT
	nextLanguage.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	nextLanguage.BorderSizePixel = 0
	nextLanguage.AutoButtonColor = false
	nextLanguage.ZIndex = 503
	nextLanguage.Parent = languageRow
	corner(nextLanguage,10)

	local countrySearch = Instance.new("TextBox")
	countrySearch.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,279)
	countrySearch.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,40)
	countrySearch.Text = ""
	countrySearch.PlaceholderText = "Search your country..."
	countrySearch.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	countrySearch.TextSize = 10
	countrySearch.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	countrySearch.TextColor3 = TEXT
	countrySearch.PlaceholderColor3 = MUTED
	countrySearch.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
	countrySearch.BorderSizePixel = 0
	countrySearch.ZIndex = 506
	countrySearch.Parent = introCard
	corner(countrySearch,10)
	addStroke(
		countrySearch,
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.2,
		.08
	)

	local searchResults = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	searchResults.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18,323)
	searchResults.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,96)
	searchResults.BackgroundColor3 = BG
	searchResults.BackgroundTransparency = .02
	searchResults.BorderSizePixel = 0
	searchResults.ScrollBarThickness = 3
	searchResults.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accent
	searchResults.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)
	searchResults.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	searchResults.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
	searchResults.Visible = false
	searchResults.ZIndex = 520
	searchResults.Parent = introCard
	corner(searchResults,10)
	addStroke(
		searchResults,
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent,
		1.2,
		.04
	)

	local resultsLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	resultsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,4)
	resultsLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	resultsLayout.Parent = searchResults

	local resultsPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	resultsPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,5)
	resultsPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0,5)
	resultsPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,5)
	resultsPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,5)
	resultsPadding.Parent = searchResults

	local enterButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
	enterButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,1)
	enterButton.Position = UDim2.new(.5,0,1,-16)
	enterButton.Size = UDim2.new(1,-36,0,48)
	enterButton.Text = "ENTER NOT RUBY"
	enterButton.TextSize = 11
	enterButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	enterButton.TextColor3 = ACTIVE_TEXT
	enterButton.BackgroundColor3 = accent
	enterButton.AutoButtonColor = false
	enterButton.ZIndex = 502
	enterButton.Parent = introCard
	corner(enterButton,12)

	local languageIndex = 1
	local searchSerial = 0

	local localeAliases = {
		-- Unsupported locales intentionally fall back to English.
	}

	local function findLanguageIndex(code)
		code = string.lower(tostring(code or "en"))
		code = string.match(code,"^[a-z]+") or "en"
		code = localeAliases[code] or code

		for index,language in ipairs(languages) do
			if language.code == code then
				return index
			end
		end

		return 1
	end

	local function normalizeSearch(value)
		value = string.lower(tostring(value or ""))
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+"," ")
		value = string.gsub(value,"^%s+","")
		value = string.gsub(value,"%s+$","")
		return value
	end

	local function clearSearchResults()
		for _,child in ipairs(searchResults:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("GuiButton") or child.Name == "NoCountryResult" then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	local function updateLanguage()
		local language = languages[languageIndex]
		if not language then
			return
		end

		local flag = countryFlag(language.countryCode)

		if visitCount > 1 then
			if language.code == "ar" then
				welcomeText.Text =
					"مرحباً بعودتك // الزيارة رقم "
					..tostring(visitCount)
			elseif language.code == "ru" then
				welcomeText.Text =
					"С ВОЗВРАЩЕНИЕМ // ВИЗИТ №"
					..tostring(visitCount)
			else
				welcomeText.Text =
					"WELCOME BACK // VISIT #"
					..tostring(visitCount)
			end
		else
			welcomeText.Text = language.welcome
		end
		welcomeSubtitle.Text = language.subtitle
		languageCaption.Text = language.language
		countrySearch.PlaceholderText = language.search

		languageButton.Text =
			flag
			.."  "
			..language.name
			.."  •  "
			..language.country
			.."  ("
			..tostring(languageIndex)
			.."/"
			..tostring(#languages)
			..")"

		enterButton.Text = language.enter

		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageCode",language.code)
		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeLanguageName",language.name)
		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeCountry",language.country)
		gui:SetAttribute("WelcomeCountryCode",language.countryCode)

		if type(writefile) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				writefile(languageFile,language.code)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function selectLanguage(index,clearSearch)
		if not languages[index] then
			return
		end

		languageIndex = index
		updateLanguage()

		if clearSearch then
			countrySearch.Text = ""
			searchResults.Visible = false
			clearSearchResults()
		end

		local scale = languageButton:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
		if not scale then
			scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
			scale.Parent = languageButton
		end

		scale.Scale = .97
		TweenService:Create(
			scale,
			TweenInfo.new(.14,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{Scale = 1}
		):Play()
	end

	local function changeLanguage(direction)
		local newIndex = languageIndex + direction

		if newIndex < 1 then
			newIndex = #languages
		elseif newIndex > #languages then
			newIndex = 1
		end

		selectLanguage(newIndex,true)
	end

	local function rebuildCountrySearch()
		searchSerial += 1
		local mySerial = searchSerial
		local query = normalizeSearch(countrySearch.Text)

		clearSearchResults()

		if query == "" then
			searchResults.Visible = false
			return
		end

		local matches = {}

		for index,language in ipairs(languages) do
			local haystack = normalizeSearch(
				language.country
				.." "
				..language.name
				.." "
				..language.code
				.." "
				..language.countryCode
				.." "
				..tostring(language.aliases or "")
			)

			if string.find(haystack,query,1,true) then
				table.insert(matches,index)
			end
		end

		if mySerial ~= searchSerial then
			return
		end

		searchResults.Visible = true

		if #matches == 0 then
			local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			empty.Name = "NoCountryResult"
			empty.Size = UDim2.new(1,-10,0,34)
			empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			empty.Text = "NO COUNTRY FOUND"
			empty.TextSize = 9
			empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			empty.TextColor3 = MUTED
			empty.ZIndex = 521
			empty.Parent = searchResults
			return
		end

		for order,index in ipairs(matches) do
			local language = languages[index]
			local result = Instance.new("TextButton")
			result.Name = "CountryResult_"..tostring(index)
			result.Size = UDim2.new(1,-10,0,36)
			result.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
			result.BorderSizePixel = 0
			result.AutoButtonColor = false
			result.Text =
				countryFlag(language.countryCode)
				.."  "
				..language.country
				.."  •  "
				..language.name
			result.TextSize = 9
			result.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			result.TextColor3 = TEXT
			result.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			result.LayoutOrder = order
			result.ZIndex = 521
			result.Parent = searchResults
			corner(result,8)

			local resultPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
			resultPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,10)
			resultPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,8)
			resultPadding.Parent = result

			result.Activated:Connect(function()
				selectLanguage(index,true)
			end)
		end
	end

	-- Auto-select a supported language from the player's Roblox locale.
	local localeOk,localeId = pcall(function()
		return LocalizationService.RobloxLocaleId
	end)

	if localeOk and localeId then
		languageIndex = findLanguageIndex(localeId)
	end

	if type(isfile) == "function"
		and type(readfile) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			if isfile(languageFile) then
				languageIndex =
					findLanguageIndex(readfile(languageFile))
			end
		end)
	end

	updateLanguage()

	previousLanguage.Activated:Connect(function()
		changeLanguage(-1)
	end)

	nextLanguage.Activated:Connect(function()
		changeLanguage(1)
	end)

	languageButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		changeLanguage(1)
	end)

	countrySearch:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		rebuildCountrySearch()
	end)

	countrySearch.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		-- Keep matching results open if there is still text so the user can tap one.
		if normalizeSearch(countrySearch.Text) == "" then
			searchResults.Visible = false
		end
	end)

	for _,button in ipairs({
		previousLanguage,
		languageButton,
		nextLanguage,
		enterButton
	}) do
		local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		scale.Parent = button

		button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(.07,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = .97}
				):Play()
			end
		end)

		button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
				or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				TweenService:Create(
					scale,
					TweenInfo.new(.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
					{Scale = 1}
				):Play()
			end
		end)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local ok,image = pcall(function()
			return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
				player.UserId,
				Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust,
				Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420
			)
		end)

		if ok and image then
			if introAvatar.Parent then
				introAvatar.Image = image
			end

			local homeAvatar =
				homePage:FindFirstChild("HomeProfileAvatar",true)

			if homeAvatar and homeAvatar:IsA("ImageLabel") then
				homeAvatar.Image = image
			end
		end
	end)

	TweenService:Create(
		introCard,
		TweenInfo.new(.30,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{Size = UDim2.fromOffset(400,445)}
	):Play()

	enterButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if entering or menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		entering = true

		local selectedLanguage = languages[languageIndex]
		if selectedLanguage then
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"WelcomeLanguageCode",
				selectedLanguage.code
			)
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"WelcomeLanguageName",
				selectedLanguage.name
			)
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"WelcomeCountry",
				selectedLanguage.country
			)
			gui:SetAttribute(
				"WelcomeCountryCode",
				selectedLanguage.countryCode
			)
		end

		panel.Visible = true
		setTab("DASHBOARD")

		local tween = TweenService:Create(
			intro,
			TweenInfo.new(.18),
			{BackgroundTransparency = 1}
		)
		tween:Play()
		tween.Completed:Wait()

		if intro then
			intro:Destroy()
		end

		setStatus(
			"READY // "
			..string.upper(player.DisplayName)
		)
	end)
end
setupProfileIntro()
applyMovementStats()
currentTab = pages["DASHBOARD"] and "DASHBOARD" or "HOME"
applyTabVisuals()
setStatus("READY")


--============================================================
-- NEW FEATURES // SHOOTER GAMES
-- Kept at the bottom of the long script as requested.
--============================================================

local function setupShooterGamesTab(shooterSecurityAllowed)
	local shooterTab = makeTab("SHOOTER","SHOOTER GAMES")
	local shooterPage = makePage("SHOOTER")
	local trollPage = pages["TROLL"]

	shooterTab.Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("SHOOTER")
	end)

	-- Keep every feature's controls in one visual block so the
	-- toggle/button, radius/power and related settings cannot drift apart.
	local function makeControlGroup(page)
		local group = Instance.new("Frame")
		group.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
		group.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		group.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		group.BorderSizePixel = 0

		local nextOrder = 0
		group.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if not child:IsA("UIListLayout") then
				nextOrder = nextOrder + 1
				child.LayoutOrder = nextOrder
			end
		end)

		group.Parent = page

		local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
		layout.Padding = UDim.new(0,8)
		layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
		layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		layout.Parent = group

		return group
	end

	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- OTHER HUBS
	-- External hubs are still protected by the same own-game PlaceId lock.
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local otherHubsTab = makeTab("OTHERHUBS","OTHER HUBS")
	local otherHubsPage = makePage("OTHERHUBS")

	otherHubsTab.Activated:Connect(function()
		setTab("OTHERHUBS")
	end)

	local otherHubsGroup = makeControlGroup(otherHubsPage)

	makeSection(
		otherHubsGroup,
		"BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB",
		"Loads Boom Empyreus's Hub. This button uses the same PlaceId lock as the protected NOT RUBY features."
	)

	local boomEmpyreusHubButton =
		makeAction(
			otherHubsGroup,
			shooterSecurityAllowed()
			and "EXECUTE BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB"
			or "BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB [LOCKED]"
		)

	boomEmpyreusHubButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			boomEmpyreusHubButton.Text =
				"BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB [LOCKED]"
			setStatus(
				"BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB // WRONG PLACEID"
			)
			return
		end

		boomEmpyreusHubButton.Text =
			"BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB [LOADING]"
		setStatus("BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB // LOADING")

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok,err = pcall(function()
				local source =
					game:HttpGet(
						"https://pastebin.com/raw/fNjdsV4F",
						true
					)

				local runner,compileError =
					loadstring(source)

				if not runner then
					error(
						compileError
						or "LOADSTRING COMPILE FAILED"
					)
				end

				runner()
			end)

			if ok then
				boomEmpyreusHubButton.Text =
					"EXECUTE BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB"
				setStatus(
					"BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB // LOADED"
				)
			else
				boomEmpyreusHubButton.Text =
					"EXECUTE BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB"
				setStatus(
					"BOOM EMPYREUS'S HUB // LOAD ERROR"
				)
				warn(
					"[NOT RUBY V1] Boom Empyreus Hub error:",
					err
				)
			end
		end)
	end)

			"SET"
		)

	local dodgeButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
		dodgeButton.Name = "NotRubyDodgeButton"
		dodgeButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
		dodgeButton.Position = UDim2.new(1,-78,.68,0)
		dodgeButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72,72)
		dodgeButton.Text = "DODGE"
		dodgeButton.TextSize = 12
		dodgeButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack

		makeNumberRow(
			shooterVisualGroup,
			"FOV CIRCLE RADIUS",
			"120",
			"SET RADIUS"
		)

	local state = {
		crosshair = false,
		fov = false,
		centerDot = false,
		teamLocator = false,
		enemyLocator = false,
		fovRadius = 120,
		aimAssist = false,
		aimbot = false,
		autoShoot = false,
		autoDodge = false,
		dodgeRadius = 18,
		dodgeDistance = 14,
		lastDodge = 0,
		selectedFlingPlayer = nil,
		flingPower = 210,
		flingActive = false,
		flingToken = 0,
		aimNoTargetReported = false,
		shootNoWeaponReported = false,
		aimStrength = .18,
		aimTarget = "Head",
		lockedTarget = nil,
		selectedAimbotPlayer = nil,
		lastAutoShot = 0
	}

	local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
	overlay.Name = "NotRubyShooterOverlay"
	overlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
	overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	overlay.ZIndex = 700
	overlay.Parent = gui

	local crosshair = Instance.new("Frame")
	crosshair.Name = "Crosshair"
	crosshair.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
	crosshair.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
	crosshair.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34,34)
	crosshair.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	crosshair.Visible = false
	crosshair.ZIndex = 701
	crosshair.Parent = overlay

	local function makeCrosshairLine(size,position)
		local line = Instance.new("Frame")
		line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
		line.Position = position
		line.Size = size
		line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
		line.BorderSizePixel = 0
		line.ZIndex = 702

	end

	makeCrosshairLine(

		UDim2.new(.5,9,.5,0)
	)
	makeCrosshairLine(

		UDim2.new(.5,0,.5,-9)
	)
	makeCrosshairLine(
		UDim2.fromOffset(2,10),
		UDim2.new(.5,0,.5,9)
	)

	local centerDot = Instance.new("Frame")

	centerDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5,5)

	centerDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
	centerDot.Visible = false
