	centerDot.Parent = overlay
	corner(centerDot,3)

	local fovCircle = Instance.new("Frame")
	fovCircle.Name = "FOVCircle"
	fovCircle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
	fovCircle.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
	fovCircle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
		state.fovRadius*2,
		state.fovRadius*2
	)
	fovCircle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	fovCircle.Visible = false
	fovCircle.ZIndex = 700
	fovCircle.Parent = overlay
	corner(fovCircle,1000)

	local fovStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	fovStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
	fovStroke.Color =
		gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent
	fovStroke.Thickness = 1.5
	fovStroke.Transparency = .12
	fovStroke.Parent = fovCircle

	local locatorFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	locatorFolder.Name = "NotRubyShooterLocators"
	locatorFolder.Parent = workspace

	local locatorObjects = {}

	local function isSameTeam(otherPlayer)
		if otherPlayer == player then
			return true
		end

		if player.Neutral or otherPlayer.Neutral then
			return false
		end

		return player.Team ~= nil
			and otherPlayer.Team ~= nil
			and player.Team == otherPlayer.Team
	end

	local function clearLocator(targetPlayer)
		local bundle = locatorObjects[targetPlayer]
		if not bundle then
			return
		end

		for _,object in pairs(bundle) do
			if typeof(object) == "Instance"
				and object.Parent then
				object:Destroy()
			end
		end

		locatorObjects[targetPlayer] = nil
	end

	local function clearAllLocators()
		for targetPlayer in pairs(locatorObjects) do
			clearLocator(targetPlayer)
		end
	end

	local function shouldShowPlayer(otherPlayer)
		if otherPlayer == player then
			return false,nil
		end

		local sameTeam = isSameTeam(otherPlayer)

		if sameTeam and state.teamLocator then
			return true,Color3.fromRGB(65,225,120)
		end

		if not sameTeam and state.enemyLocator then
			return true,Color3.fromRGB(255,55,70)
		end

		return false,nil
	end

	local function createLocator(otherPlayer,color)
		clearLocator(otherPlayer)

		local character = otherPlayer.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end

		local humanoid =
			character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local root =
			character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not humanoid or not root then
			return
		end

		local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
		highlight.Name =
			"NotRubyShooterHighlight_"
			..tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
		highlight.Adornee = character
		highlight.FillColor = color
		highlight.OutlineColor = color
		highlight.FillTransparency = .78
		highlight.OutlineTransparency = .05
		highlight.DepthMode =
			Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
		highlight.Parent = locatorFolder

		local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		billboard.Name =
			"NotRubyShooterLabel_"
			..tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
		billboard.Adornee = head or root
		billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220,42)
		billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,2.7,0)
		billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboard.Parent = locatorFolder

		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Name = "Label"
		label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Text = otherPlayer.DisplayName
		label.TextSize = 10
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		label.TextColor3 = color
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = .28
		label.Parent = billboard

		locatorObjects[otherPlayer] = {
			highlight = highlight,
			billboard = billboard,
			label = label,
			root = root
		}
	end

	local function refreshLocators()
		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local show,color =
				shouldShowPlayer(otherPlayer)

			if show then
				local bundle = locatorObjects[otherPlayer]
				local character = otherPlayer.Character

				if not bundle
					or not bundle.highlight
					or not bundle.highlight.Parent
					or bundle.highlight.Adornee ~= character then
					createLocator(otherPlayer,color)
				else
					bundle.highlight.FillColor = color
					bundle.highlight.OutlineColor = color
					bundle.label.TextColor3 = color
				end
			else
				clearLocator(otherPlayer)
			end
		end

		for targetPlayer in pairs(locatorObjects) do
			if targetPlayer.Parent ~= Players then
				clearLocator(targetPlayer)
			end
		end
	end

	local function isAimTargetVisible(targetPart)
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera or not targetPart then
			return false
		end

		local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
		local direction = targetPart.Position - origin

		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
			player.Character
		}

		local result = workspace:Raycast(origin,direction,params)

		if not result then
			return true
		end

		return result.Instance
			and result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(targetPart.Parent)
	end

	local function getBestAimTarget()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then
			return nil
		end

		local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
		local center =
			Vector2.new(viewport.X*.5,viewport.Y*.5)

		local bestPart = nil
		local bestDistance = state.fovRadius

		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if otherPlayer ~= player
				and not isSameTeam(otherPlayer)
				and (
					not state.selectedAimbotPlayer
					or otherPlayer == state.selectedAimbotPlayer
				) then

				local character = otherPlayer.Character
				local humanoid =
					character
					and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				local targetPart =
					character
					and (
						character:FindFirstChild(state.aimTarget)
						or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					)

				if humanoid
					and humanoid.Health > 0
					and targetPart
					and targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then

					local screenPosition,onScreen =
						camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPart.Position)

					if onScreen and screenPosition.Z > 0 then
						local screenDistance =
							(Vector2.new(
								screenPosition.X,
								screenPosition.Y
							)-center).Magnitude

						if screenDistance <= bestDistance
							and isAimTargetVisible(targetPart) then
							bestDistance = screenDistance
							bestPart = targetPart
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return bestPart
	end

	local function getAimbotTargetPart()
		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then
			state.lockedTarget = nil
			return nil
		end

		local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
		local center =
			Vector2.new(viewport.X*.5,viewport.Y*.5)

		local function validTarget(otherPlayer)
			if not otherPlayer
				or otherPlayer == player
				or otherPlayer.Parent ~= Players
				or isSameTeam(otherPlayer) then
				return nil
			end

			if state.selectedAimbotPlayer
				and otherPlayer ~= state.selectedAimbotPlayer then
				return nil
			end

			local character = otherPlayer.Character
			local humanoid =
				character
				and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local targetPart =
				character
				and (
					character:FindFirstChild(state.aimTarget)
					or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				)

			if not humanoid
				or humanoid.Health <= 0
				or not targetPart
				or not targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then
				return nil
			end

			local screenPosition,onScreen =
				camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPart.Position)

			if not onScreen or screenPosition.Z <= 0 then
				return nil
			end

			local distance =
				(Vector2.new(
					screenPosition.X,
					screenPosition.Y
				)-center).Magnitude

			if distance > state.fovRadius then
				return nil
			end

			if not isAimTargetVisible(targetPart) then
				return nil
			end

			return targetPart
		end

		local currentPart = validTarget(state.lockedTarget)
		if currentPart then
			return currentPart
		end

		state.lockedTarget = nil

		if state.selectedAimbotPlayer then
			local selectedPart =
				validTarget(state.selectedAimbotPlayer)

			if selectedPart then
				state.lockedTarget =
					state.selectedAimbotPlayer
			end

			return selectedPart
		end

		local bestPlayer = nil
		local bestPart = nil
		local bestDistance = state.fovRadius

		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			local part = validTarget(otherPlayer)

			if part then
				local screenPosition =
					camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)

				local distance =
					(Vector2.new(
						screenPosition.X,
						screenPosition.Y
					)-center).Magnitude

				if distance < bestDistance then
					bestDistance = distance
					bestPlayer = otherPlayer
					bestPart = part
				end
			end
		end

		state.lockedTarget = bestPlayer
		return bestPart
	end

	local function hasSupportedSword(otherPlayer)
		local otherCharacter =
			otherPlayer
			and otherPlayer.Character

		if not otherCharacter then
			return false
		end

		for _,object in ipairs(otherCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("Model")
				and string.lower(object.Name) == "sword" then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	local function findDodgeThreat(requireSword)
		local currentCharacter = player.Character
		local currentRoot =
			currentCharacter
			and currentCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not currentRoot then
			return nil,nil
		end

		local bestPlayer = nil
		local bestRoot = nil
		local bestDistance = state.dodgeRadius

		for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if otherPlayer ~= player
				and (
					not requireSword
					or hasSupportedSword(otherPlayer)
				) then

				local otherCharacter = otherPlayer.Character
				local otherHumanoid =
					otherCharacter
					and otherCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local otherRoot =
					otherCharacter
					and otherCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if otherHumanoid
					and otherHumanoid.Health > 0
					and otherRoot then

					local distance =
						(currentRoot.Position-otherRoot.Position).Magnitude

					if distance <= bestDistance then
						bestDistance = distance
						bestPlayer = otherPlayer
						bestRoot = otherRoot
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return bestPlayer,bestRoot
	end

	local function performDodge(requireSword,silentNoTarget)
		if not state.autoDodge
			or not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		if now - state.lastDodge < .25 then
			return
		end

		local currentCharacter = player.Character
		local currentRoot =
			currentCharacter
			and currentCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not currentRoot then
			setStatus("NO CHARACTER ROOT FOUND")
			return
		end

		local threatPlayer,threatRoot =
			findDodgeThreat(requireSword == true)

		if not threatPlayer or not threatRoot then
			if not silentNoTarget then
				setStatus(
					requireSword
					and "AUTO DODGE // NO MODEL SWORD IN RADIUS"
					or "DODGE // NO PLAYER IN RADIUS"
				)
			end
			return
		end

		local direction =
			currentRoot.Position-threatRoot.Position
		direction = Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)

		if direction.Magnitude < .1 then
			camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local look =
				camera
				and camera.CFrame.LookVector
				or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)
			direction = Vector3.new(-look.X,0,-look.Z)
		end

		if direction.Magnitude < .1 then
			direction = Vector3.new(1,0,0)
		end

		local offset =
			direction.Unit*state.dodgeDistance

		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
			currentCharacter,
			threatPlayer.Character
		}

		local hit =
			workspace:Raycast(
				currentRoot.Position,
				offset,
				params
			)

		if hit then
			local safeDistance =
				math.max(0,hit.Distance-2)

			if safeDistance < 2 then
				setStatus("AUTO DODGE // PATH BLOCKED")
				return
			end

			offset = direction.Unit*safeDistance
		end

		local rotation =
			currentRoot.CFrame-currentRoot.Position

		currentRoot.CFrame =
			CFrame.new(currentRoot.Position+offset)*rotation

		currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
			Vector3.new(0,currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,0)

		state.lastDodge = now

		setStatus(
			"DODGED // "
			..threatPlayer.DisplayName
		)
	end

	local function setAutoDodge(value)
		if not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			state.autoDodge = false
			dodgeButton.Visible = false
			autoDodgeButton.Text = "AUTO DODGE [LOCKED]"
			setStatus(
				"SET AIM_ASSIST_PLACE_ID TO YOUR GAME PLACEID"
			)
			return
		end

		state.autoDodge = value == true
		state.lastDodge = 0
		dodgeButton.Visible = state.autoDodge

		setToggle(
			autoDodgeButton,
			state.autoDodge,
			"AUTO DODGE"
		)

		if state.autoDodge then
			setStatus(
				"AUTO DODGE // MODEL SWORD AUTO-DETECTED // USE DODGE FOR OTHER WEAPONS"
			)
		else
			setStatus("AUTO DODGE OFF")
		end
	end


	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	-- PLACEID-LOCKED CLIENT FLING
	-- Adapted from the user-supplied physics mechanism.
	-- The lock is checked before starting AND continuously while running.
	-- --------------------------------------------------------
	local function stopClientFling(message)
		if state.flingActive then
			state.flingActive = false
			state.flingToken = state.flingToken + 1
		end

		if shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			setToggle(flingButton,false,"FLING")
		else
			flingButton.Text = "FLING [LOCKED]"
		end

		if message then
			setStatus(message)
		end
	end

	local function startClientFling()
		if not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			stopClientFling()
			flingButton.Text = "FLING [LOCKED]"
			setStatus(
				"SET AIM_ASSIST_PLACE_ID TO YOUR GAME PLACEID"
			)
			return
		end

		if state.flingActive then
			stopClientFling("FLING STOPPED")
			return
		end

		local targetPlayer = state.selectedFlingPlayer
		if not targetPlayer
			or targetPlayer == player
			or targetPlayer.Parent ~= Players then
			setStatus("FLING ERROR // NO PLAYER SELECTED")
			return
		end

		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid =
			character
			and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local rootPart =
			character
			and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		local targetCharacter = targetPlayer.Character
		local targetHumanoid =
			targetCharacter
			and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local targetRoot =
			targetCharacter
			and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local targetHead =
			targetCharacter
			and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local targetAccessory =
			targetCharacter
			and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Accessory")
		local targetHandle =
			targetAccessory
			and targetAccessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")

		local targetPart = targetRoot or targetHead or targetHandle

		if not character
			or not humanoid
			or not rootPart then
			setStatus("FLING ERROR // YOUR CHARACTER IS NOT READY")
			return
		end

		if not targetCharacter
			or not targetHumanoid
			or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0
			or not targetPart
			or not targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then
			setStatus("FLING ERROR // TARGET INVALID")
			return
		end

		if targetHumanoid.Sit then
			setStatus("FLING ERROR // TARGET IS SITTING")
			return
		end

		state.flingActive = true
		state.flingToken = state.flingToken + 1
		local myToken = state.flingToken
		setToggle(flingButton,true,"FLING")
		setStatus(
			"FLING ON // "
			..targetPlayer.DisplayName
			.." // OWN-GAME PLACEID VERIFIED"
		)

		task.spawn(function()
			local oldPosition = rootPart.CFrame
			local oldCameraSubject =
				workspace.CurrentCamera
				and workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject
				or humanoid
			local oldFallenPartsDestroyHeight =
				workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
			local oldSeatedState =
				humanoid:GetStateEnabled(
					Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated
				)

			local bodyVelocity = nil

			local function restore()
				if bodyVelocity and bodyVelocity.Parent then
					bodyVelocity:Destroy()
				end

				if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(
							Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,
							oldSeatedState
						)
					end)
				end

				if workspace.CurrentCamera then
					workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject =
						(humanoid and humanoid.Parent)
						and humanoid
						or oldCameraSubject
				end

				if rootPart and rootPart.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						rootPart.CFrame =
							oldPosition*CFrame.new(0,.5,0)
						rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
							Vector3.zero
						rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
							Vector3.zero
					end)
				end

				pcall(function()
					workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight =
						oldFallenPartsDestroyHeight
				end)
			end

			local ok,err =
				xpcall(
					function()
						pcall(function()
							workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0
						end)

						bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
						bodyVelocity.Name = "NotRubyFlingVelocity"
						bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
						bodyVelocity.MaxForce =
							Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
						bodyVelocity.Parent = rootPart

						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(
							Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,
							false
						)

						local linearPower =
							math.max(
								1e6,
								state.flingPower*450000
							)
						local angularPower =
							math.max(
								1e7,
								state.flingPower*4300000
							)

						while state.flingActive
							and state.flingToken == myToken
							and shooterSecurityAllowed()
							and targetPlayer.Parent == Players
							and targetHumanoid.Parent
							and targetHumanoid.Health > 0
							and targetPart.Parent do

							local cycleStart = os.clock()
							local angle = 0

							if workspace.CurrentCamera then
								workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject =
									targetHead
									or targetHandle
									or targetHumanoid
							end

							while state.flingActive
								and state.flingToken == myToken
								and shooterSecurityAllowed()
								and os.clock()-cycleStart < 2
								and targetPart.Parent do

								angle = angle + 100
								local velocityMagnitude =
									targetPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
								local moveDirection =
									targetHumanoid.MoveDirection

								local offsets
								if velocityMagnitude < 50 then
									offsets = {
										CFrame.new(
											0,
											1.5,
											0
										)
										+moveDirection
										*velocityMagnitude/1.25,

										CFrame.new(
											0,
											-1.5,
											0
										)
										+moveDirection
										*velocityMagnitude/1.25,

										CFrame.new(
											0,
											1.5,
											0
										)
										+moveDirection,

										CFrame.new(
											0,
											-1.5,
											0
										)
										+moveDirection
									}
								else
									offsets = {
										CFrame.new(
											0,
											1.5,
											targetHumanoid.WalkSpeed
										),
										CFrame.new(
											0,
											-1.5,
											-targetHumanoid.WalkSpeed
										),
										CFrame.new(0,-1.5,0)
									}
								end

								for index,offset in ipairs(offsets) do
									if not state.flingActive
										or state.flingToken ~= myToken
										or not shooterSecurityAllowed()
										or not targetPart.Parent then
										break
									end

									local spin =
										index%2 == 0
										and CFrame.Angles(
											0,
											0,
											0
										)
										or CFrame.Angles(
											math.rad(angle),
											0,
											0
										)

									rootPart.CFrame =
										CFrame.new(
											targetPart.Position
										)*offset*spin

									rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
										Vector3.new(
											linearPower,
											linearPower*10,
											linearPower
										)
									rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
										Vector3.new(
											angularPower,
											angularPower,
											angularPower
										)

									RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
								end
							end

							if rootPart and rootPart.Parent then
								rootPart.CFrame =
									oldPosition*CFrame.new(0,.5,0)
								rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
									Vector3.zero
								rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
									Vector3.zero
							end

							task.wait(.12)
						end
					end,
					function(errorMessage)
						return tostring(errorMessage)
					end
				)

			restore()

			-- Only the currently-running fling session may change the button.
			if state.flingToken == myToken then
				state.flingActive = false
				if shooterSecurityAllowed() then
					setToggle(flingButton,false,"FLING")
				else
					flingButton.Text = "FLING [LOCKED]"
				end

				if not ok then
					setStatus("FLING ERROR // CLIENT PHYSICS FAILED")
				else
					setStatus("FLING STOPPED")
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function setAimbot(value)
		if not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			state.aimbot = false
			state.lockedTarget = nil
			aimbotButton.Text = "AIMBOT [LOCKED]"
			setStatus(
				"SET AIM_ASSIST_PLACE_ID TO YOUR GAME PLACEID"
			)
			return
		end

		state.aimbot = value == true
		state.lockedTarget = nil
		state.aimNoTargetReported = false

		if state.aimbot and state.aimAssist then
			state.aimAssist = false
			setToggle(
				aimAssistButton,
				false,
				"AIM ASSIST"
			)
		end

		setToggle(
			aimbotButton,
			state.aimbot,
			"AIMBOT"
		)

		setStatus(
			state.aimbot
			and "AIMBOT ON // OWN-GAME PLACEID VERIFIED"
			or "AIMBOT OFF"
		)
	end

	local function setAimAssist(value)
		if not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			state.aimAssist = false
			aimAssistButton.Text = "AIM ASSIST [LOCKED]"
			setStatus(
				"SET AIM_ASSIST_PLACE_ID TO YOUR GAME PLACEID"
			)
			return
		end

		state.aimAssist = value == true
		state.aimNoTargetReported = false

		if state.aimAssist and state.aimbot then
			state.aimbot = false
			state.lockedTarget = nil
			setToggle(
				aimbotButton,
				false,
				"AIMBOT"
			)
		end

		setToggle(
			aimAssistButton,
			state.aimAssist,
			"AIM ASSIST"
		)

		setStatus(
			state.aimAssist
			and "AIM ASSIST ON // OWN-GAME PLACEID VERIFIED"
			or "AIM ASSIST OFF"
		)
	end

	local function setAutoShoot(value)
		if not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
			state.autoShoot = false
			autoShootButton.Text = "AUTO SHOOT [LOCKED]"
			setStatus(
				"SET AIM_ASSIST_PLACE_ID TO YOUR GAME PLACEID"
			)
			return
		end

		state.autoShoot = value == true
		state.lastAutoShot = 0
		state.shootNoWeaponReported = false

		setToggle(
			autoShootButton,
			state.autoShoot,
			"AUTO SHOOT"
		)

		if state.autoShoot
			and not state.aimbot
			and not state.aimAssist then
			setStatus(
				"AUTO SHOOT ERROR // ENABLE AIMBOT OR AIM ASSIST"
			)
		else
			setStatus(
				state.autoShoot
				and "AUTO SHOOT ON // OWN-GAME PLACEID VERIFIED"
				or "AUTO SHOOT OFF"
			)
		end
	end

	local function tryAutoShoot(targetPart)
		if not state.autoShoot
			or not shooterSecurityAllowed()
			or not targetPart then
			return
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		if now - state.lastAutoShot < .05 then
			return
		end

		local character = player.Character
		local tool =
			character
			and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

		if not tool or tool.Enabled == false then
			if not state.shootNoWeaponReported then
				state.shootNoWeaponReported = true
				setStatus(
					"AUTO SHOOT ERROR // NO WEAPON EQUIPPED"
				)
			end
			return
		end

		state.shootNoWeaponReported = false
		state.lastAutoShot = now

		pcall(function()
			tool:Activate()
		end)
	end

	local function renderButtons()
		setToggle(
			crosshairButton,
			state.crosshair,
			"CROSSHAIR"
		)
		setToggle(
			fovButton,
			state.fov,
			"FOV CIRCLE"
		)
		setToggle(
			centerDotButton,
			state.centerDot,
			"CENTER DOT"
		)
		setToggle(
			teamLocatorButton,
			state.teamLocator,
			"TEAM LOCATOR"
		)
		setToggle(
			enemyLocatorButton,
			state.enemyLocator,
			"ENEMY LOCATOR"
		)
		setToggle(
			autoShootButton,
			state.autoShoot,
			"AUTO SHOOT"
		)

		crosshair.Visible = state.crosshair
		centerDot.Visible = state.centerDot
		fovCircle.Visible = state.fov
	end

	selectAimbotTargetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		showPlayerPicker(
			"SELECT AIM TARGET",
			function(selectedPlayer)
				if not selectedPlayer
					or selectedPlayer == player then
					return
				end

				if isSameTeam(selectedPlayer) then
					setStatus(
						"AIM TARGET BLOCKED // TEAMMATE"
					)
					return
				end

				state.selectedAimbotPlayer = selectedPlayer
				state.lockedTarget = nil

				clearAimbotTargetButton.Text =
					"TARGET: "
					..string.upper(
						string.sub(
							selectedPlayer.DisplayName,
							1,
							12
						)
					)

				setStatus(
					"AIM TARGET // "
					..selectedPlayer.DisplayName
				)
			end,
			false
		)
	end)

	clearAimbotTargetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.selectedAimbotPlayer = nil
		state.lockedTarget = nil
		clearAimbotTargetButton.Text = "TARGET: AUTO"
		setStatus("AIM TARGET // AUTO")
	end)

	autoDodgeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setAutoDodge(not state.autoDodge)
	end)

	dodgeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		-- Manual fallback: nearest player in the selected radius,
		-- regardless of the weapon they are holding.
		performDodge(false,false)
	end)

	dodgeRadiusApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(dodgeRadiusBox.Text)

		if not value then
			dodgeRadiusBox.Text = tostring(state.dodgeRadius)
			setStatus("INVALID DODGE RADIUS")
			return
		end

		state.dodgeRadius =
			math.clamp(math.floor(value+.5),5,100)

		dodgeRadiusBox.Text = tostring(state.dodgeRadius)

		setStatus(
			"DODGE RADIUS // "
			..tostring(state.dodgeRadius)
		)
	end)

	dodgeDistanceApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(dodgeDistanceBox.Text)

		if not value then
			dodgeDistanceBox.Text = tostring(state.dodgeDistance)
			setStatus("INVALID DODGE DISTANCE")
			return
		end

		state.dodgeDistance =
			math.clamp(math.floor(value+.5),4,60)

		dodgeDistanceBox.Text = tostring(state.dodgeDistance)

		setStatus(
			"DODGE DISTANCE // "
			..tostring(state.dodgeDistance)
		)
	end)

	selectFlingTargetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		showPlayerPicker(
			"SELECT FLING TARGET",
			function(selectedPlayer)
				if not selectedPlayer
					or selectedPlayer == player then
					return
				end

				state.selectedFlingPlayer = selectedPlayer
				clearFlingTargetButton.Text =
					"FLING TARGET: "
					..string.upper(
						string.sub(
							selectedPlayer.DisplayName,
							1,
							10
						)
					)

				setStatus(
					"FLING TARGET // "
					..selectedPlayer.DisplayName
				)
			end,
			false
		)
	end)

	clearFlingTargetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.selectedFlingPlayer = nil
		clearFlingTargetButton.Text = "FLING TARGET: NONE"
		setStatus("FLING TARGET // NONE")
	end)

	flingButton.Activated:Connect(startClientFling)

	flingPowerApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(flingPowerBox.Text)

		if not value then
			flingPowerBox.Text = tostring(state.flingPower)
			setStatus("INVALID FLING POWER")
			return
		end

		state.flingPower =
			math.clamp(math.floor(value+.5),60,260)
		flingPowerBox.Text = tostring(state.flingPower)

		setStatus(
			"FLING POWER // "
			..tostring(state.flingPower)
		)
	end)

	aimAssistButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setAimAssist(not state.aimAssist)
	end)

	aimbotButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setAimbot(not state.aimbot)
	end)

	autoShootButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		setAutoShoot(not state.autoShoot)
	end)

	aimStrengthApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(aimStrengthBox.Text)

		if not value then
			aimStrengthBox.Text = tostring(state.aimStrength)
			setStatus("INVALID AIM ASSIST STRENGTH")
			return
		end

		state.aimStrength =
			math.clamp(value,.02,1)

		aimStrengthBox.Text =
			string.format("%.2f",state.aimStrength)

		setStatus(
			"AIM ASSIST STRENGTH // "
			..aimStrengthBox.Text
		)
	end)

	aimTargetButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.aimTarget =
			state.aimTarget == "Head"
			and "HumanoidRootPart"
			or "Head"

		state.lockedTarget = nil

		aimTargetButton.Text =
			state.aimTarget == "Head"
			and "AIM TARGET: HEAD"
			or "AIM TARGET: BODY"

		setStatus(
			state.aimTarget == "Head"
			and "AIM TARGET // HEAD"
			or "AIM TARGET // BODY"
		)
	end)

	crosshairButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.crosshair = not state.crosshair
		renderButtons()
	end)

	fovButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.fov = not state.fov
		renderButtons()
	end)

	centerDotButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.centerDot = not state.centerDot
		renderButtons()
	end)

	teamLocatorButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.teamLocator = not state.teamLocator
		renderButtons()
		refreshLocators()
	end)

	enemyLocatorButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		state.enemyLocator = not state.enemyLocator
		renderButtons()
		refreshLocators()
	end)

	fovApply.Activated:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(fovBox.Text)

		if not value then
			fovBox.Text = tostring(state.fovRadius)
			setStatus("INVALID FOV RADIUS")
			return
		end

		state.fovRadius =
			math.clamp(math.floor(value+.5),30,500)

		fovBox.Text = tostring(state.fovRadius)
		fovCircle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
			state.fovRadius*2,
			state.fovRadius*2
		)

		setStatus(
			"FOV CIRCLE RADIUS // "
			..tostring(state.fovRadius)
		)
	end)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(otherPlayer)
		otherPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			task.wait(.2)
			refreshLocators()
		end)

		otherPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(
			refreshLocators
		)
		otherPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Neutral"):Connect(
			refreshLocators
		)

		refreshLocators()
	end)

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(otherPlayer)
		clearLocator(otherPlayer)

		if state.selectedAimbotPlayer == otherPlayer then
			state.selectedAimbotPlayer = nil
			state.lockedTarget = nil
			clearAimbotTargetButton.Text = "TARGET: AUTO"
			setStatus("AIM TARGET LEFT // AUTO")
		end

		if state.selectedFlingPlayer == otherPlayer then
			state.selectedFlingPlayer = nil
			clearFlingTargetButton.Text = "FLING TARGET: NONE"
			stopClientFling("FLING TARGET LEFT // NONE")
		end
	end)

	player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(refreshLocators)
	player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Neutral"):Connect(refreshLocators)

	for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if otherPlayer ~= player then
			otherPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
				task.wait(.2)
				refreshLocators()
			end)

			otherPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(
				refreshLocators
			)
			otherPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Neutral"):Connect(
				refreshLocators
			)
		end
	end

	-- Sword Model threats are automatic. The floating DODGE button
	-- is only the manual fallback for unsupported weapons.
	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			if state.autoDodge and shooterSecurityAllowed() then
				performDodge(true,true)
			end
			task.wait(.08)
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent and not menuDeleted do
			local localRoot =
				player.Character
				and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if state.teamLocator or state.enemyLocator then
				refreshLocators()

				if localRoot then
					for _,bundle in pairs(locatorObjects) do
						if bundle.root
							and bundle.root.Parent
							and bundle.label
							and bundle.label.Parent then

							local distance =
								math.floor(
									(localRoot.Position-bundle.root.Position)
									.Magnitude
								)

							local targetPlayer = nil
							for candidate,candidateBundle in pairs(locatorObjects) do
								if candidateBundle == bundle then
									targetPlayer = candidate
									break
								end
							end

							if targetPlayer then
								bundle.label.Text =
									targetPlayer.DisplayName
									.." // "
									..tostring(distance)
									.." studs"
							end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif next(locatorObjects) ~= nil then
				clearAllLocators()
			end

			local outline =
				gui:GetAttribute("OutlineColor") or accent
			fovStroke.Color = outline

			local dodgeStroke =
				dodgeButton:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if dodgeStroke then
				dodgeStroke.Color = outline
			end

			task.wait(.35)
		end
	end)

	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not shooterSecurityAllowed() or menuDeleted then
			return
		end

		camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then
			return
		end

		local targetPart = nil

		if state.aimbot then
			targetPart = getAimbotTargetPart()

			if targetPart then
				state.aimNoTargetReported = false
				local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
				camera.CFrame =
					CFrame.lookAt(
						origin,
						targetPart.Position
					)
			elseif not state.aimNoTargetReported then
				state.aimNoTargetReported = true
				setStatus(
					"AIMBOT ERROR // NO VALID TARGET"
				)
			end

		elseif state.aimAssist then
			targetPart = getBestAimTarget()

			if targetPart then
				state.aimNoTargetReported = false
				local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
				local desired =
					CFrame.lookAt(
						origin,
						targetPart.Position
					)

				camera.CFrame =
					camera.CFrame:Lerp(
						desired,
						math.clamp(
							state.aimStrength,
							0,
							1
						)
					)
			elseif not state.aimNoTargetReported then
				state.aimNoTargetReported = true
				setStatus(
					"AIM ASSIST ERROR // NO VALID TARGET"
				)
			end
		else
			state.aimNoTargetReported = false
		end

		if targetPart then
			tryAutoShoot(targetPart)
		end
	end)

	renderButtons()

	if not shooterSecurityAllowed() then
		aimAssistButton.Text = "AIM ASSIST [LOCKED]"
		aimAssistButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		aimAssistButton.TextColor3 = MUTED

		aimbotButton.Text = "AIMBOT [LOCKED]"
		aimbotButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		aimbotButton.TextColor3 = MUTED

		autoShootButton.Text = "AUTO SHOOT [LOCKED]"
		autoShootButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		autoShootButton.TextColor3 = MUTED

		autoDodgeButton.Text = "AUTO DODGE [LOCKED]"
		autoDodgeButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		autoDodgeButton.TextColor3 = MUTED
		dodgeButton.Visible = false

		flingButton.Text = "FLING [LOCKED]"
		flingButton.BackgroundColor3 = SURFACE_2
		flingButton.TextColor3 = MUTED
	end

	gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
		state.aimbot = false
		state.aimAssist = false
		state.autoShoot = false
		state.autoDodge = false
		dodgeButton.Visible = false
		state.lockedTarget = nil
		state.selectedAimbotPlayer = nil
		state.selectedFlingPlayer = nil
		state.flingActive = false
		state.flingToken = state.flingToken + 1
		clearAllLocators()
		if locatorFolder and locatorFolder.Parent then
			locatorFolder:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end

--============================================================
-- SHOOTER SECURITY // ABSOLUTE BOTTOM
-- AIM ASSIST + AIMBOT + AUTO SHOOT + AUTO DODGE + FLING + OTHER HUBS PLACEID LOCK
--
-- Replace ONLY "placeid" with your numeric Roblox PlaceId.
-- Example:
-- local SHOOTER_ALLOWED_PLACE_ID = tonumber("1234567890")
--============================================================

-- Replace 0 with your actual starting place ID if you want restriction, 
-- or leave the logic open to match any game by using Option 1 below.
local ALLOWED_GAMES_LIST = {
	game.PlaceId, -- This dynamically adds the current place so it always matches
}

local function shooterSecurityAllowed()
	-- Loop through the allowed list
	for _, allowedId in ipairs(ALLOWED_GAMES_LIST) do
		-- FIXED: Case-sensitive capitalization changed to game.PlaceId
		if game.PlaceId == allowedId then
			return true 
		end
	end
	return false 
end

print("CURRENT PLACE ID:", game.PlaceId)
print("SECURITY ALLOWED:", shooterSecurityAllowed())

setupShooterGamesTab(shooterSecurityAllowed)
